## astral_rift.2000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在一个废弃的研究设施内。\n\n这个房间的表面覆盖着厚厚的灰尘，表明它已经有一段时间没有使用了。\n\n我们无法找到任何研究人员，无论是活着与否。无论这个地方的看守者是谁，他们早已离开了。\n\n墙壁上排列着重型架子，每个架子上都放着一枚大型蛋。它们中的大多数都处于不同的腐烂状态，但仍有一些活体标本。\n\n在这个房间的中心有一个敞开的容器，里面装满了苍白的粘性液体。对液体的分析显示，它含有大量的蛋白质和氨基酸。从一些蛋上留下的残留物来看，我们推测这些液体是由研究人员注射的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:7`

### English

We are inside of an abandoned research facility.\n\nThe surfaces in this room are coated in a thick layer of dust, indicating it has not been in use for some time.\n\nWe can detect no researchers, living or otherwise. Whoever the caretakers of this place might have been, they are long since gone.\n\nHeavy-duty shelves line the walls, each holding a large egg. Most of them are in various states of decay, but a few living specimens remain.\n\nAt the center of this chamber rests an open container filled with a pale, viscous liquid. An analysis of the fluid reveals that it contains high amounts of proteins and amino acids. Judging by the residue left on some of the eggs, we speculate this fluid was being injected by the researchers.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:9`

## astral_rift.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们砸开了蛋中看起来最健康的样本。\n\n里面的胚胎看起来营养不良，并被外来寄生虫所覆盖。\n\n值得注意的是，这些寄生虫拥有任何已知生物体所不曾拥有的独特的量子特性。它们在蛋壳内部随机传送，甚至可以传送到胚胎内部。鉴于该实验室中只剩下很少的活的蛋，这个特殊的标本必然对寄生虫的行为具有特别的抵抗力。\n\n对这两种生物体的星界追踪表明，这些寄生虫起源于一个独特且遥远的维度。我们相信这种寄生虫是无意中被召唤到这里的，并中断了该设施的预期研究。\n\n我们看到他们被这个房间中央的一桶奇怪液体所吸引。也许这就是吸引他们来到这里的首要原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:11`

### English

We have opened the healthiest looking specimen among the eggs.\n\nThe embryo inside appeared malnourished and covered with foreign parasites.\n\nRemarkably, the parasites possess quantum properties unique to any known biological organism. They warp randomly around the inside of the egg's casing, and presumably inside of the embryo itself. Given that so few living eggs remain in this laboratory, this particular specimen must be especially resistant to the parasite's behavior.\n\nAstral tracing of both organisms indicate the parasites originated from a distinct and distant dimension. We believe this parasite was inadvertently summoned here, and has interrupted the intended research of this facility.\n\nWe have seen them drawn to the vat of strange liquid in the center of this chamber. Perhaps this is what attracted them here in the first place.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:14`

## astral_rift.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到了这个地方的看护者。更准确地说，我们已经找到了它们的残骸。\n\n附近的一个房间里堆满了尸体。他们的四肢已经被某种未知的力量在原子层面上彻底地分开了。有些尸体半融合在墙内。\n\n中央检查台上放着一堆大部分被解剖过的蛋。其中一枚完好无损。\n\n将看护者的遗体带回进行适当的研究应该可以让我们对这里发生的事情有一些洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

We have found the caretakers of this place. More accurately, we have found what remains of them.\n\nA nearby room is littered with corpses. Their limbs have been cleanly separated at the atomic level by some unknown force. Some of the carcasses are half-fused inside of the walls.\n\nA clutch of mostly dissected eggs is present on a central examining table. One of remains intact.\n\nTaking the caretakers' remains back for proper study should provide some insight into what happened here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:19`

## astral_rift.2020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将苍白液体注入暴露的胚胎中产生了一些有趣的结果。\n\n尽管有量子寄生体，之前昏昏欲睡的样本已然重新焕发了活力。事实上，我们可能错误地计算了剂量。在很短的时间内，一个发育更完全的生物开始触碰蛋壳——蛋壳上覆盖着多层密布的装甲板和尖刺。\n\n然而，寄生虫也受到刺激。当它们积极地传送进出胚胎时，该生物的组织就会受到损害。按照这个速度，它很可能会死亡。\n\n我们推测，注入少量的星体丝缕可能会满足量子寄生虫并防止进一步的损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

Injecting the pale liquid into the exposed embryo has produced some interesting results.\n\nIn spite of the quantum parasites, the previously lethargic specimen has become reinvigorated. In fact, we may have miscalculated the dose. In a very short period of time, a more developed creature began pushing at the walls of the egg - one covered in a lattice of overlapping armored plates and spines.\n\nHowever, the parasites were also stimulated. As they energetically teleport in and out of the embryo, the creature's tissue is being damaged. At this rate, it will likely perish.\n\nWe speculate that injecting a small amount of astral thread may satisfy the quantum parasites and prevent further damage.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:24`

## astral_rift.2025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个生物没有康复的希望。感染它的蛋的寄生虫太危险了，不应该传播到这个裂隙之外。\n\n按照严格的防污染流程，我们用盐酸对附近的所有标本进行了安乐死。我们将带着样品返回以供进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

This creature had no hope of recovery. The parasites infesting its egg are too dangerous to allow propagation beyond this rift.\n\nFollowing strict anti-contamination procedures, we have euthanized all specimens in the vicinity with hydrochloric acid. We are returning with samples for further study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:29`

## astral_rift.2030.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2030.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

这种不稳定的量子感染一定破坏了这个实验室本来要进行的任何实验。\n\n尽管如此，我们在这里的观察增进了我们对极其不稳定条件下的生物学的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

This volatile quantum infection must have disrupted whatever experiments were meant to be happening in this laboratory.\n\nNonetheless, our observations here have advanced our understanding of biology in extremely unstable conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:34`

## astral_rift.2030.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2030.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

胚胎已经解体。\n\n果然，它体内的寄生体很快就将它朝着随机的方向分裂开来。\n\n$astral_rift.2030.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

The embryo has disintegrated.\n\nAs expected, the parasites in its body soon split it apart in random directions.\n\n$astral_rift.2030.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:35`

## astral_rift.2035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

试验成功了。\n\n我们向胚胎中注入的星界丝缕将量子寄生虫稳定住了。\n\n发育完全的幼雏完好无损地破壳而出。它意外地具有服从性，而且很快地学会了基本的命令。基于这点，再加上它厚厚的盔甲般的皮毛和有毒的刺，我们推测这些生物是为了战斗而繁育的。\n\n令人惊奇的是，它们还展现出了短距离曲速跃迁的能力。星界丝缕似乎把这些生物和寄生虫被编织成了某种特别的共生体。\n\n我们可能创造出了某种特别危险的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

The experiment was a success.\n\nThe astral thread we have injected into the embryo has stabilized the quantum parasites.\n\nThe fully-developed hatchling has emerged intact. It is incredibly obedient and was quick to learn the most basic of commands. Based on that, as well as its thick, armored-hide and poisonous spines, we speculate that these creatures were being bred for war. \n\nStartlingly, it has demonstrated the ability to "warp" small distances. The astral thread seems to have woven a unique symbiotic relationship between the creature and the parasites.\n\nWe may have created something entirely too dangerous.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:41`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完成了研究人员尸体的采集工作，并将其运回来作进一步研究。\n\n他们身上长满了量子寄生虫，这些寄生虫具有任何已知生物有机体所不具有的特性。它们不可预测地“扭曲”物质的能力可能是导致宿主死亡的原因。\n\n两种生物体的星界追踪表明寄生虫起明显源于一个的遥远的次元。我们认为这种寄生虫是无意中被召唤到这里的，它以一种毁灭性的方式中断了该设施的原定研究。\n\n我们已用氢氯酸迅速消灭了害虫，以防止进一步污染。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

We've completed the harvesting of the researcher's corpses and are transporting them back for further study.\n\nThey were covered with quantum parasites that possess properties unique to any known biological organism. Their ability to unpredictably 'warp' through matter is likely what resulted in the death of their hosts.\n\nAstral tracing of both organisms indicate the parasites originated from a distinct and distant dimension. We believe this parasite was inadvertently summoned here, and has interrupted the intended research of this facility in a devastating fashion.\n\nWe have summarily neutralized the pests with hydrochloric acid to prevent further contamination.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:48`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2040.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人着迷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

Fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:49`

## astral_rift.2045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]突然去世了。死因是暴露在来自[FromFrom.solar_system.GetName]裂隙彼端的致命寄生虫。他的研究部门也被隔离，以待进一步的消杀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

[From.GetName] was found dead. The cause was exposure to the deadly parasites beyond the rift in [FromFrom.solar_system.GetName]. Their quarters are quarantined and awaiting full decontamination.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:52`

The Mechanism

## astral_rift.2100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

齿轮与链条

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

Gears and Pinions

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:60`

## astral_rift.2100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们被巨大的机械结构包围了。\n\n齿轮和链条在我们的四面八方转动着。每过十分之一秒，机械就往前搏动一刻。齿轮上的每一个齿几乎都达到了我们§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的宽度。\n\n这一切都坐落在无边无际的稠密气体和浓雾的星云中央，无始无终。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

We are surrounded by giant machinery.\n\nGears and pinions twist in all directions. Each tenth of a second, the machine clicks one step forward. A single tooth from one of these gears is nearly the width of our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nAmidst swirling nebulae of dense gas and fog, there is no beginning or end to the device in sight.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:61`

## astral_rift.2100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

探索迷雾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

Explore the fog.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:62`

## astral_rift.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

次元机械

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

Dimensional Machine

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:64`

## astral_rift.2105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这台次元机械运转得和我们最精密的时钟一样准确。\n\n尽管我们还搞不明白它的动力来源，但是稍加探索我们就发现了通向一系列星界裂隙的竖井。\n\n无论这里到底是什么东西，它的建造应该具有某种目的。这台机器可以延伸至许多的宇宙。\n\n巨大部件的运动在稠密大气中造成了湍流，使我们的仪器难以稳定。通过一些基本的操作，我们可以松开其中一个轴，用它来卡住一对旋转的齿轮。这样可以使环境平息下来，我们才能更仔细地研究这台令人印象深刻的机器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

The movement of this dimensional machine is as precise as our most accurate clocks.\n\nThough we have not been able to determine its power source, with some exploring we have found shafts extending into a series of Astral Rifts.\n\nWhatever this place is, it has been made with a purpose in mind. This machine is reaching into many universes. \n\nThe movement of its massive components are creating turbulence in the dense atmosphere, making it difficult to steady our instruments. With some rudimentary manipulation, we could slip one of the shafts loose and use it to jam a pair of the rotating cogs. This would quiet the agitation of the atmosphere, allowing for a more intimate study of this impressive machinery.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:65`

## astral_rift.2105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

干涉可能是不负责任的行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

Interfering would be irresponsible.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:66`

## astral_rift.2105.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2105.b&lang=en)

### 中文

卡住齿轮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

Jam the gears.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:67`

## astral_rift.2110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

如果没有坏……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

If It Ain't Broke...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:69`

## astral_rift.2110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个空间的建造者拥有远远超出我们的知识。干扰这些机制可能会产生难以预测的后果。\n\n尽管受到雾的干扰，我们还是在这里逗留了足够长的时间，获得了关于这台巨大机器的精确构造的有用的工程见解，并收集了相当数量的星界物质。\n\n维护这个空间的人一定是伟大的建筑师。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

The architects of this space have knowledge far beyond our own. Interfering with these mechanisms could have wildly unpredictable consequences.\n\nDespite the disruptions from the fog, we have lingered long enough to gain useful engineering insight on the precise construction of this massive machinery, as well as collected a sizable amount of astral material.\n\nWhoever maintains this space must be grand architects indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:70`

## astral_rift.2115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

卡住

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Jammed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:72`

## astral_rift.2115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

齿轮被卡住了。\n\n随着一阵剧烈的震动，整台机器停了下来。我们的破坏企图成功了。\n\n随着张力的增加，一个金色的核心从一块网状板底下暴露出来。它光洁如新，但只有很短的时间可以接触它。逐渐增大的张力必然将不可避免突破障碍。我们已经开始了提取核心的精细过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

The gears have jammed.\n\nWith a great shudder, the entire mechanism came to a halt. Our attempt at sabotage was successful.\n\nAs the tension grew, a golden core was exposed under one of the reticulated plates. Glowing and pristine, it will only be accessible for a short time. Inevitably, the growing tension will clear the blockage. We have already begun the delicate process of the core's extraction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:73`

## astral_rift.2115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

小心行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

Use caution.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:74`

## astral_rift.2120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

Shatter

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:76`

## astral_rift.2120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图取出金色核心时，它的表面迅速地蔓延开了一道裂痕。我们还没来得及把它取出来，它就猛烈地向四面八方炸开了。\n\n核心破碎的时候释放出强烈的能量。能量冲破了障碍，使我们周围巨大的齿轮开始疯狂运转。\n\n我们已经启动了紧急锚链来逃离这个不幸的情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

As we attempted to recover the golden core, a small crack quickly propagated throughout its surface. Before we could complete its retrieval, it exploded violently forth in all directions.\n\nAs it shattered, it released an intense energy. Overcoming our blockage, the massive gears around us began spinning wildly out of control.\n\nWe have activated the emergency anchor cable to escape this unfortunate situation.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:77`

## astral_rift.2125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:62`

### English

Success

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:79`

## astral_rift.2125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地取回了其中一台机器上的金色核心。\n\n周围的齿轮陷入了诡异的沉默，四周原有的张力也一扫而空。\n\n连接到这台装置的星界裂隙网络肉眼可见地停止了微微的震颤，仿佛时间静止了一般。我们无法确定这么做会有什么后果。\n\n我们成功地拖动了锚链离开了这个空间。取回的核心具有显著的时间特性，我们才刚刚开始了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:63`

### English

We have recovered the golden core of one of the mechanisms.\n\nThe surrounding gears now lay oddly silent. All tension around us has been relieved.\n\nThe network of Astral Rifts connected to this device have visibly ceased their subtle movements, as if stopped in time. Of what consequence this is, we cannot be sure.\n\nWe have safely retracted our anchor cable and left this space. The recovered core has remarkable temporal properties which we are only beginning to understand.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:80`

## astral_rift_dimensional_dump

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift_dimensional_dump&lang=en)

维度垃圾场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

Dimensional Dump

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:85`

## astral_rift.2200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮躯体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

Floating Body

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:87`

## astral_rift.2200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们被垃圾包围了。\n\n时不时就有新裂缝在我们周围的太空中打开，并向旋转的垃圾堆中倾倒更多的垃圾。根据碎片的性质，我们判定这里是一个星际文明的垃圾场。未知机器的零件和碎片与外星家庭的日常一次性用品掺杂散落在一起。\n\n一般来说，这些物品的价值与废料无异。然而，我们的传感器却在其中发现了生命的迹象。\n\n一个穿着宇航服的未知生物的处于休眠状态。目前还不清楚它在这里漂浮了多久。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

We are surrounded by garbage.\n\nFrom time to time, we have witnessed new tears in space open around us and dump even more garbage into the swirling heap. Based on the nature of the debris, this space is being treated as a junkyard for a star-faring civilization. Parts and pieces of unknown machines are littered amongst the everyday disposables of alien households.\n\nGenerally, these items are of no greater value than scrap. However, our sensors have picked up signs of life.\n\nInside of a spacesuit, the unconscious body of an unknown organism lies dormant. It is unclear how long it has been floating here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:88`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2200.a&lang=en)

### English

带回来拿走它的装备。

### English

Bring it aboard to recover its gear.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2205.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

把那个漂浮在垃圾堆里的外星人带回来是个错误的决定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

Taking in the alien found floating within the garbage was a mistake.

## astral_rift.2205.common.2

### 中文

他说着一种我们无法理解的语言，来来回回地指着球体的控制面板，反复强调着同一个短语。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

They speak a language we do not understand and routinely point to the Sphere's controls, emphatically repeating the same phrase.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:94`

## astral_rift.2205.desc.help

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2205.desc.help&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2205.common.1$\n\n在恢复了他的核心体温，稳定了生命体征后，这个外星人很快就复苏了——他满脸困惑，神经紧绷。\n\n他很快抓住了一位我们医疗团队的人员，用靴子中抽出的武器把他们劫为人质。$astral_rift.2205.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

$astral_rift.2205.common.1$\n\nAfter restoring its core temperature and tending to its vitals, the alien revived quickly - confused, and alarmed.\n\nIt quickly grabbed a member of our medical team and is currently holding them hostage with a weapon drawn from their boot.$astral_rift.2205.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:95`

## astral_rift.2205.desc.recover_gear

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2205.desc.recover_gear&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2205.common.1$\n\n是这件宇航服让这个外星人保持休眠的。一经脱下，这个生物很快就苏醒了——他满脸困惑，神经紧绷。\n\n他很快抓住了一位我们医疗团队的人员，用靴子中抽出的武器把他们劫为人质。$astral_rift.2205.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

$astral_rift.2205.common.1$\n\nIts spacesuit kept the alien in hibernation. After removal, the creature revived quickly - confused, and alarmed.\n\nIt quickly grabbed a nearby welding torch and is currently holding a member of our engineering team hostage.$astral_rift.2205.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:96`

## astral_rift.2205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

干掉他。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

Dispatch the individual.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:97`

## astral_rift.2210.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2210.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

总的来说，我们干掉了这个从垃圾堆里拖回来的好斗生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

We have summarily dispatched the belligerent creature we've rescued from this trash dump.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:101`

## astral_rift.2210.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2210.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们也就有了充足的时间去分析他的装备中携带的文件。初步翻译表明，他的身份是一名资深垃圾搬运工。他应该是把自己不小心混进了垃圾堆里，再也回不了家了。\n\n暂且不提那个生物，我们会留在裂隙里，确认并回收尽可能多有价值的废料才回去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

We have had time to analyze the documents it held in its gear. Early translations of its identifications indicate it was a veteran trash hauler. It must have accidentally dumped itself into this garbage heap along with its load, never to return home.\n\nIgnoring the creature for now, we will be remaining the rift until we have finished identifying and recovering as much valuable scrap as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:102`

## astral_rift.2210.desc.help

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2210.desc.help&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2210.common.1$\n\n虽然人质受了一些轻微的烧伤，但我们保安人员的迅速行动避免了进一步的伤害。\n\n$astral_rift.2210.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

$astral_rift.2210.common.1$\n\nThough the hostage sustained some minor burn injuries, the quick actions of our security officer prevented any further damage.\n\n$astral_rift.2210.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:103`

## astral_rift.2210.desc.recover_gear

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2210.desc.recover_gear&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2210.common.1$\n\n因为他的装备已经被剥离，对我们来说用普通的等离子子弹来终结他就是小菜一碟。\n\n$astral_rift.2210.common.2$\n\n至于我们取回的他的服装具有高度先进的构造，我们打算留着进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

$astral_rift.2210.common.1$\n\nCompletely stripped of gear, it was no great difficulty for us to neutralize it with a simple plasma round.\n\n$astral_rift.2210.common.2$\n\nThe suit we recovered is of a highly advanced construction which we plan on keeping for further study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:104`

真是个可怜蛋。

### English

Unfortunate for them.

### 中文

缓和

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

De-escalation

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:107`

## astral_rift.2215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为表善意，我们放下了手里的武器。作为回应，这位外星人也松开了我们的[Explorer.GetCrewMember]，并同样放下武器。他看起来情绪十分地激动复杂，不过在这种情况也是可以理解的。\n\n给了他一点冷静的时间，他开始愿意与我们沟通了。\n\n我们开始寻找一种适当的方式来进行对话。虽然进展缓慢，但从其掌握的文件的初步翻译来看，这个外星人已经有几百岁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

Demonstrating our peaceful intentions, we laid down our weapons. The alien released its grip on our [Explorer.GetCrewMember] and dropped their weapon in return. It appears to be in a highly emotional state, as is to be expected under the current circumstances.\n\nGiven some time to calm down, it became receptive to our communications.\n\nWe have begun the process of finding a way to hold a proper conversation. Progress has been slow, but early translations of the documents found in its possession indicate the alien is hundreds of years old.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:108`

## astral_rift.2215.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2215.a&lang=en)

好极了。

### English

Excellent.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭受伤亡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

Fatalities Sustained

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:111`

## astral_rift.2220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们彻底结果掉这个敌意的外星人之前，他率先从靴子中抽出一把等离子手枪，杀死了人质和其他几名船员。\n\n不幸的是，[Leader.GetName]也在被害者列表之中。\n\n我们拉动了应急锚链，救回了伤者。\n\n我们需要花一些时间来分析这个外星人的太空服。它是用高度先进的合金构造，应该会很有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Before we could neutralize the hostile alien, it dispatched their hostage and several others with a powerful plasma pistol drawn from their boot.\n\nUnfortunately, [Leader.GetName] was among the list of casualties.\n\nWe are pulling emergency anchor and returning with the wounded.\n\nWe have taken the time to analyze the spacesuit of the recovered alien. It is constructed of a highly advanced alloy which should prove useful.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:112`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2225.name&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

Lost Junker

## astral_rift.2225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位外星人的名字叫[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetName]。\n\n有一次在往这个次元倾倒垃圾的途中，[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe]无意间被卷入了事故。[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHisHer]舰船的次元引擎发生了一些技术故障需要手动维修，而[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe]则是唯一一个具有维修技术的人。在维修的过程中，[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe]不小心滑入了时空压缩器中，然后失踪了。\n\n[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHisHer]先进的宇航服中的生命支持系统使[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHimHer]保持昏迷超过两个世纪以上。\n\n虽然[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetName]悲伤地思念着家人，但[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHisHer]也知道我们没有办法把[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe]送回[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHimHer]来的地方。\n\n[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe]具有驾驶先进舰船的极高技艺，应该能很好地适应我们的舰队训练项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

The alien's name is [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetName].\n\nOn a routine haul to dump a load of trash into this dimension, [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe] was inadvertently sent along for the ride. A technical issue with the dimensional drive aboard [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHisHer] ship required manual repair, and [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe] was the only one with the technical skill to fix it. In doing so, [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe] accidentally slipped into the space-time compressor and was lost.\n\nThe life support systems of [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHisHer] advanced spacesuit kept [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHimHer] in hibernation for over two centuries.\n\n[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetName] grievously miss[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetEsXX] [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHisHer] family, but know[astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetSXX] we have no means to return [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHimHer] from whence [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe] came.\n\nHaving notable skills in piloting advanced craft, [astral_rift_dimensional_dump_leader_loc.GetHeShe] would fit nicely into our fleet training program.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:115`

## astral_rift.2230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过严格的训练后，[From.GetName]不仅证明了自己是一位出色的飞行员，还取得了我们军官们的信任。[From.GetHeSheCap]可以成为一名出色的指挥官，现已入役等待指派。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

After extensive training, [From.GetName] [From.GetHasHave] proven to be an excellent pilot and [From.GetHasHave] earned the confidence of our officers. [From.GetHeSheCap] will make an excellent Commander and [From.GetHasHave] been promoted to active service, awaiting assignment.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:120`

## astral_rift.2300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此处的环境构成是非物质的。简单的扫描表明，整个空间都是某个未知来源的全息投影。\n\n在我们抵达不久之后就收到了一份用我们语言发出的通讯。我们面前的空间凝聚成了一行文字。\n\n§E“我不认识你。”§!\n\n在我们解释了自己的来历之后，这个实体继续说：\n\n§E“真有趣。那很好。我们玩个游戏吧。我来当创世神。”§!\n\n全息投影组成了新的画面。在一颗荒芜的星球上，一个[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种正在挣扎求生。它正在向我们展示一个幼年文明的诞生。\n\n§E“我们要对他们施加何种赐福呢？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

The composition of this environment is immaterial. A simple scan revealed that the entire space is a holographic projection from some unknown source.\n\nSoon after our arrival, we received a communication in our own language. The space in front of us resolved into a single line of text.\n\n§E"I do not know you."§!\n\nAfter we explained our presence, the entity continued:\n\n§E"How curious. Very well then. Let us play a game. I will take the role of Genesis."§!\n\nThe hologram shifted into a new arrangement. On a desolate world, a [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species struggles to survive. We are being shown the beginnings of a young civilization.\n\n§E"What blessing should we grant them?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:131`

## astral_rift.2300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2300.a&lang=en)

以力量武装他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Arm them with might.

We play no games. Activate an E.M.P.

## astral_rift.2305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2305.name&lang=en)

力量投射

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:136`

## astral_rift.2305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只一瞬间，这个全息文明演化了数个世纪。\n\n§E“我们的子民变得强大了。”§!\n\n这个发展中的文明使用新获得的力量来征服自然世界。他们自称为[genesis_new_species_base.GetName]。\n\n§E“俗话说独木难支。你们认为他们对未来最好应该有何准备？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

In an instant, the holographic civilization advanced through centuries of time.\n\n§E"Our people have grown powerful."§!\n\nThe developing civilization has used its new-found strength to dominate the natural world. They call themselves the [genesis_new_species_base.GetName].\n\n§E"But a people cannot prosper by strength alone. How best should they prepare for the future?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:137`

## astral_rift.2305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2305.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Through diligence and hard work.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:138`

### English

## astral_rift.2310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

智慧之果

### English

Fruit of Knowledge

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:141`

## astral_rift.2310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只一瞬间，这个全息文明演化了数个世纪。\n\n§E“我们的子民变得聪颖了。”§!\n\n这个发展中的文明使用新获得的智慧来操控自然世界。他们自称为[genesis_new_species_base.GetName]。\n\n§E“没有方向的智慧没有意义。我们应该给他们铺上一条什么道路？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

In an instant, the holographic civilization advanced through centuries of time.\n\n§E"Our people are clever."§!\n\nThe developing civilization has used its intelligence to manipulate the natural world. They call themselves the [genesis_new_species_base.GetName].\n\n§E"But intelligence without direction is meaningless. What path shall we lay for them?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:142`

## astral_rift.2315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们通过锚链释放了一道强力的电磁脉冲，全息环境瞬间溶解了。\n\n“创世神”陷入了沉默。结果，这个实体不过是一种先进的中央处理器。我们发现它的核心静静地漂浮在虚空中。我们把它带上了船，准备带回进行进一步研究。\n\n无论这个地方是用来干什么的，它都不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

We have released a powerful electromagnetic pulse via our anchor cable, dissolving the holographic environment.\n\n"Genesis" has gone silent. In the end, the entity was nothing more than an advanced central processing unit. We found its inert core floating motionless in the void. We have brought it aboard, and will take it back for further study.\n\nWhatever this place was, it is no more.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:147`

### 中文

新生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

New Life

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:175`

## astral_rift.2330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§E“我们的模拟完成了。你的选择已经被记录。感谢你的参与。”§!\n\n“创世神”停止了一切通讯，我们被强制弹回了我们的维度。我们更关心的是突然出现在我们身边的一群陌生人。\n\n他们的外表和举止与全息图中的人一模一样，热情地欢迎我们。快速医学扫描显示他们是现实生物——毫无疑问，他们是生物。他们是如何形成的，以及他们是如何被运送到球体上的，仍然是一个谜。\n\n现在，我们将把他们安置在附近的一个行星上，[genesis_new_species_selected_planet.GetName]。在那里，我们可以继续观察他们的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

§E"Our simulation is complete. Your choices have been logged. Thank you for your participation."§!\n\n"Genesis" has ceased all communications, and we have been forcibly ejected back into our dimension. Of more immediate concern are the strangers we discovered along with us.\n\nIdentical in appearance and bearing to those witnessed in the hologram, they greeted us warmly. A quick medical scan revealed their biological reality - there is no doubt that these are living beings. How they came to be, and how they were transported aboard the Sphere, remains a mystery.\n\nFor now, we will settle them on a nearby planet, [genesis_new_species_selected_planet.GetName]. There, we can continue to observe their development.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:176`

## leader_trait_foreign_consciousness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_foreign_consciousness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖为一个裂隙中出生的灵能实体提供了附身的容器，能够更好地理解星界位面中怪异的物理学。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

### English

## astral_rift.2400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇怪的网

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Strange Mesh

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:187`

## astral_rift.2400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现自己身处一张巨大的网中。能量沿着网格状的墙壁有节奏地噼啪作响。\n\n一团浓缩的暗物质云悬浮在这张纠结的网的深处。半透明的卷须向外探测能量屏障，接触后会产生火花，然后反冲回来。幽灵般的粒子像有规律地运动着。且不论这团幽灵般的物质是什么，它对我们的存在是没有反应的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

We find ourselves inside of an enormous mesh. Energy crackles rhythmically along its gridlike walls.\n\nA concentrated cloud of dark matter is suspended deep within this tangled web. Translucent tendrils probe outward against the energy barrier, sparking on contact before recoiling back. The ghostly particles move as if patterned. Whatever this spectral mass may be, it has not reacted to our presence.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:188`

## astral_rift.2400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

分析这张网。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

Examine the mesh.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:189`

## astral_rift.2400.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2400.b&lang=en)

### 中文

接近暗物质团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

Approach the mass of dark matter.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:190`

## astral_rift.2405.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2405.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有限电流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Limited Current

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:192`

## astral_rift.2405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这张网覆盖了巨大的面积，完全暴露在周围的空间中。建造它所使用的技术尚不为人所知，但可以合理地假设，它的设计目的是拦截和捕获非固体材料。\n\n当我们环视四周时，只有那个云团在微微颤动。它是网格中唯一的其他存在。\n\n在我们继续观察的过程中，我们观察到一种从矩阵延伸出来的装置。它似乎限制了流入网格的电流的强度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

Covering an enormous surface area, the mesh is fully exposed to the surrounding space. The technology used in its construction is unfamiliar, but it is reasonable to assume that it was designed to intercept and capture non-solid material.\n\nThe amorphous cloud stirred slightly as we surveyed our surroundings. It is the only other presence within the mesh.\n\nAs we continued our observations, a mechanism was observed extending from the matrix. It seems to be limiting the amplitude of the current flowing into the mesh.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:193`

## astral_rift.2405.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2405.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

### English

## astral_rift.2410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

心灵感应通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Telepathic Communication

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:197`

## astral_rift.2410.desc.common.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2410.desc.common.default&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们接近那团虚无缥缈的物质时，我们开始在意识中感受到某种存在。看似随机的知识片段被挖掘出来，旧的记忆也被唤起。尽管有侵入性的存在，但我们没有感觉到恶意或不适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

As we approached the ethereal mass, we began to feel a presence in our minds. Seemingly random bits of knowledge were dredged up, and old memories stirred. Despite the invasive presence, we sensed no malicious intent or discomfort.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:198`

## astral_rift.2410.desc.common.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2410.desc.common.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们接近那团虚无缥缈的物质时，我们的[Explorer.GetNetworkName]中探测到一个源自[Leader.GetName]的独立但良性的意识。它似乎正在我们整个集体知识和记忆中搜索着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

As we approached the ethereal mass, an independent yet benign consciousness was detected in our [Explorer.GetNetworkName] originating from [Leader.GetName]. It appeared to be searching through our collective knowledge and memories.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:199`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

The entity stirred, and a message appeared in our minds: §E"I have never encountered your kind before. What are you?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:200`

## astral_rift.2415.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2415.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不祥提案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

An Ominous Proposal

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:212`

## astral_rift.2415.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2415.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

该实体听取了我们的答复，于是另一条信息响起：§E“又一个好奇的物种，很高兴见到你们。”\n\n“我的同类构成了现实的基石。我们也是流浪者和探索者，对体验宇宙有着极大的兴趣。遗憾的是，另一种生命形式却只是把我们当做物质矿藏；一种随手可得的能量来源，可以随意抓捕和收割。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

The entity considered our response. Another message rang out: §E"Another curious species, it is fascinating to meet you."\n\n"My kind makes up the building blocks of reality. We are also wanderers and explorers who take great interest in experiencing the universe. Regrettably, another lifeform saw us as a mere material deposit; a passing fuel source to be captured and harvested."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:213`

## astral_rift.2415.desc.common.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2415.desc.common.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§E“我希望能借用[Leader.GetName]的身体。这样就可以把我从这个牢笼中释放出去。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

§E"I wish to inhabit the body of [Leader.GetName]. Doing so would free me from this prison."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:214`

## astral_rift.2415.desc.common.non_psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2415.desc.common.non_psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

§E“如果你允许我占有这个容器，我将用我的知识和精神力量来执行你的人民的意志，直到永远。不幸的是，我不确定他的意识是否强大到足以与我共存。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

§E"If you allow me to possess this vessel, I will use my depth of knowledge and psychic prowess to carry out your people's will for eternity. Unfortunately I am not sure their mind is powerful enough to coexist with my consciousness."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:215`

## astral_rift.2415.desc.common.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2415.desc.common.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

§E“我们可以共存，我们的实力相结合足以将科学推向新的边界。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

§E"We will coexist, with our combined prowess we will push our scientific boundaries to new heights."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:216`

## astral_rift.2415.desc.common.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2415.desc.common.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§E“在你们的帮助下我可以逃出这个监狱。你们的物种与我不谋而合，我只希望能加入你们的集体意识。我会把我自己的意识融入整个[Leader.Species.GetBrainJunctionName]当中，在所有科学的领域中增进洞见。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

§E"With your help, I can escape this prison. Your kind is compatible with my intentions, I wish only to join your collective consciousness. I will diffuse my mind across your [Leader.Species.GetBrainJunctionName] and enhance your insight in all scientific endeavors."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:217`

## astral_rift.2420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

囚徒的请求

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

A Prisoner's Plea

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:227`

## astral_rift.2420.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2420.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

这个虚无缥缈的生物对我们的拒绝做出了激烈的反应，它以狂暴的光谱卷须和火花猛烈地拍打着网状结构。\n\n几分钟后，一切平静下来。精疲力竭的实体重新打开了通信。§E”行吧。如果你不希望我的加入，那么至少赐给我死亡的仁慈。”§!\n\n它用卷须做了个手势，继续说，§E“关闭这个装置会激活这个网格，让我的粒子失去活性。被囚禁的日子是一点也过不下去了。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

The ethereal creature reacted violently to our rejection, lashing out against the meshed structure in a furious wave of spectral tendrils and sparks.\n\nAfter a few minutes, it settled. The exhausted entity reopened communications. §E"Very well. If you do not wish for me to join you, then at least give me the mercy of death."§!\n\nGesturing with a tendril, it continued, §E"Deactivating this mechanism will energize this mesh, rendering my particles inert. A life imprisoned is no life at all."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:228`

## astral_rift.2420.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2420.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一声歇斯底里的哀号穿过内部通讯的本地线路。\n\n$astral_rift.2420.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

A primordial wail traversed local lines of internal communication.\n\n$astral_rift.2420.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:229`

## astral_rift.2425.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2425.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们关闭了中央的装置，一道高能脉冲席卷整个网络内部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

We deactivated the central mechanism, sending waves of high energy coursing through the internals of the mesh.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:239`

## astral_rift.2425.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2425.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

随着暗物质席卷而来焚毁了整个充能网络，中央的物质团失去了光芒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

The central mass grew faint as dark matter surged and burned against the energized mesh.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:240`

## astral_rift.2425.desc.common.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2425.desc.common.3&lang=en)

### 中文

于是火花四溅，暗物质向外泼洒而出，在§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!上覆盖了薄薄的一层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

The spark then burst and dark matter showered outward, coating the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! in a fine film.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:241`

## astral_rift.2425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.1$\n\n卷须从幽灵般的云状物中分裂出来，拼命地冲破能量屏障。$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.2$\n\n一道强烈的蓝色火花点燃了整个结构的中心，把这些颗粒压缩成了原来尺寸的很小一部分。$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.1$\n\nTendrils splintered from the spectral cloud, desperately thrusting against the energy barrier. $astral_rift.2425.desc.common.2$\n\nA violent blue spark ignited in the center of the structure, condensing the particles into a fraction of their original size. $astral_rift.2425.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:242`

## astral_rift.2426.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2426.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.1$\n\n卷须从幽灵般的生物中分裂出来，拼命地冲破能量屏障。$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.2$\n\n一道强烈的蓝色火花点燃了整个结构的中心，把这个实体压缩成了原来尺寸的很小一部分。$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

$astral_rift.2425.desc.common.1$\n\nTendrils splintered from the spectral creature, desperately thrusting against the energy barrier. $astral_rift.2425.desc.common.2$\n\nA violent blue spark ignited in the center of the structure, condensing the entity into a fraction of their original size. $astral_rift.2425.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:243`

## astral_rift.2430.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2430.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

这个幽魂般的物质团陷入了沉默，然后平铺到了整个网络上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Now fully inert, the spectral mass diffused evenly throughout the mesh.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:250`

## astral_rift.2430.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2430.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]被绑在诊断台上，意识转移开始了。他不安地扭动着身体，外来的意识逐渐淹没大脑，导致了无法控制的痉挛。幽灵般的实体变成了不稳定能量的漩涡。突然间，一切归于平静，[Leader.GetName]以一种新的姿态站了起来。\n\n$astral_rift.2430.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

[Leader.GetName] was bound to a diagnostic bench, and the transfer began. They writhed with discomfort, spasming uncontrollably as the foreign consciousness overwhelmed their mind. The ghostly entity became a churning vortex of unstable energy. Suddenly, everything went quiet, and [Leader.GetName] rose with a new demeanor.\n\n$astral_rift.2430.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:251`

## astral_rift.2431.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2431.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

幽灵般的实体变成了不稳定能量的漩涡。[Leader.GetName]不安地扭动着身体，无法控制地痉挛。突然，他站了起来，脸上带着茫然而又沉思的表情。短暂的停顿之后，他向团队的其他成员做了一个[Leader.GetName]的手势，并示意返回科研船。\n\n$astral_rift.2430.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

The ghostly entity became a churning vortex of unstable energy. [Leader.GetName] writhed with discomfort, spasming uncontrollably. Suddenly, they rose with a blank yet pensive expression. After a brief pause, they acknowledged the rest of the team with a gesture of their [Leader.GetName] and motioned to return to the Science vessel.\n\n$astral_rift.2430.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:252`

## astral_rift.2430.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2430.a&lang=en)

欢迎加入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2435.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的难题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Unfortunate Complications

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:255`

## astral_rift.2435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]被绑在诊断台上，意识转移开始了。他不安地扭动着身体，外来的意识尝试占据大脑，导致了无法控制的痉挛。然而，[Leader.GetName]挣脱了束缚，取出了隐藏的武器，紧接着就结束了自己的生命，过程中一同带走那个虚无缥缈的生物。\n\n团队里剩下的人试着在这个空间中收集有用的材料，包括大量暗物质，然后启程返航。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

[Leader.GetName] was bound to a diagnostic bench, and the transfer began. They writhed with discomfort, spasming uncontrollably as the foreign consciousness attempted to occupy their mind. However, [Leader.GetName] managed to escape their restraints and recover a concealed weapon. They subsequently took their own life, killing both themselves and the ethereal creature in the process.\n\nThe remaining team attempted to gather whatever other resources were available within this space before returning, including a significant amount of dark matter.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:256`

## astral_rift.2440.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2440.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

这个幽魂般的物质团陷入了沉默，然后平铺到了整个网络上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Now fully inert, the spectral mass diffused evenly throughout the mesh.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:258`

## astral_rift.2440.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2440.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们把它的意识吸收进我们的意识织构内，这个虚无缥缈的实体开始向自身内部旋转和皱缩。几千年的知识和经验流入了我们更大的意识之中。\n\n$astral_rift.2440.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

The ethereal entity swirled and thrashed within itself as we absorbed its consciousness into the mind weave. Millennia of knowledge and observations entered our greater consciousness.\n\n$astral_rift.2440.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:259`

## astral_rift.2440.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2440.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

数据传输开始后，这个虚无缥缈的实体也开始向自身内部旋转和皱缩。几千年的知识和经验流入了我们的网络之中。基于这些信息的优化也开始实装。\n\n$astral_rift.2440.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

The ethereal entity swirled and thrashed within itself as the stream of data began. Millennia of knowledge and observations entered the network. Optimizations based on this information were implemented accordingly.\n\n$astral_rift.2440.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:260`

## astral_rift.2445.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2445.name&lang=en)

### 中文

笼中鸟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Life in a Cage

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:262`

## astral_rift.2445.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2445.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

暗物质的每一根卷须都缩回了它的中心的物质团。\n\n我们已经部署了无人侦察机和数据基础设施来对这个装置和它的囚犯进行交叉观察。\n\n从我们从裂隙撤退到现在已经过去好几天了，那个实体仍旧一动不动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Every tendril of its dark matter withdrew back into its central mass.\n\nWe have deployed surveillance drones and data infrastructure for cross-rift observation of this device and its prisoner.\n\nDays have passed since our exit back through the rift, and the entity still has yet to move.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:263`

## astral_rift.2445.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2445.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一做出明确的决定，这个虚无缥缈的生物立刻就建立了意识连接。$astral_rift.2445.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Once our decision was made clear, the ethereal creature immediately severed the mental connection. $astral_rift.2445.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:264`

## astral_rift.2445.desc.robotic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2445.desc.robotic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一做出明确的决定，这个虚无缥缈的生物立刻就建立了通讯线路。$astral_rift.2445.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Once our decision was made clear, the ethereal creature immediately severed lines of communication. $astral_rift.2445.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:265`

## astral_rift.2445.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2445.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是个诡异的标本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

A peculiar specimen.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:266`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2450.name&lang=en)

### 中文

研究客体安乐死

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

Research Subject Euthanized

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:268`

## astral_rift.2450.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2450.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.System.GetName]的裂隙另一头研究的实体被它所处的维度中的某个未知操作者摧毁了。我们收集的研究数据到此为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

The entity on the side of the rift we were studying in [From.System.GetName] has been neutralized by an unknown actor within its own dimension. The research data we were collecting has ceased.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:269`

## leader_trait_black_light_blinded

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_black_light_blinded&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Black Light Blinded

## leader_trait_black_light_blinded_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_black_light_blinded_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖被星界位面中的黑暗星星刺瞎了，但获得了对知识的无尽饥渴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

Blinded by a dark sun in the astral planes, this leader has gained an insatiable hunger for knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:275`

## astral_rift_bleached_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift_bleached_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

褪色行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

Bleached Planet

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:277`

## astral_rift.2500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灰色环境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

Ashen Environment

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:279`

## astral_rift.2500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们进入了一颗陆地星球的大气层。这颗行星看起来可能适合居住，但地形和树叶明显发生了褪色，就好像星球表面和上面的一切都被漂白了一样。\n\n尽管有明显的植物群存在，但船上的传感器显示没有生命迹象。对这个行星生态系统的彻底分析需要进行直接的生物调查。\n\n我们找到了一个可行的着陆点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

We have entered the atmosphere of a continental world. The planet looks potentially habitable, however the terrain and foliage have significant discoloration. It is as if the surface and everything on it has been bleached white.\n\nDespite the obvious presence of flora, onboard sensors indicate no signs of life. A thorough analysis of the planet's ecosystem will require a direct biological survey.\n\nWe have located a potential landing site.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:280`

## astral_rift.2500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

探索整个地貌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Explore the terrain.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:281`

## astral_rift.2505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

褪色地景

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

Bleached Landscape

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:283`

## astral_rift.2505.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2505.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们走下§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!，四散开调查周围的环境。土壤由许多脆性层组成，在压力下会发生分解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

Leaving the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!, we spread out to investigate the surroundings. The soil consists of a number of brittle layers, which disintegrate under pressure.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:284`

## astral_rift.2505.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2505.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的植物也非常脆弱，即使是最轻微的接触也会导致破碎。我们看见地上长出嫩芽，树枝上也长出嫩芽。有明显的生命迹象，但我们的仪器显示不存在。\n\n随着调查的继续，我们开始发现地下有动静。根据它目前的轨迹，无论来袭的是什么，都将在不到一小时内突破地表。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

The plant life is similarly fragile, crumbling at even the slightest touch. We have observed sprouts jutting from the ground, and the presence of buds on the branches. There are clear signs of life, but our instruments continue to deny it.\n\nAs the survey continued, we began to detect movement underground. Based on its current trajectory, whatever is coming will breach the surface in less than an hour.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:285`

## astral_rift.2505.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2505.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.1$\n\n主要矿物质仅有痕量存在，但很难想象在这样的条件下有什么东西能生长。\n\n$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.1$\n\nThere are trace amounts of core minerals, but it is difficult to imagine how anything can grow under such conditions.\n\n$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:286`

## astral_rift.2505.desc.plantoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2505.desc.plantoid&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.1$\n\n这种土壤几乎无法维持种子生长，更不用说破土发芽了。\n\n$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.1$\n\nThis soil should barely sustain a seedling, let alone a budding ground.\n\n$astral_rift.2505.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:287`

## astral_rift.2510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

石质生物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

Rock Creatures

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:291`

## astral_rift.2510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着地下活动越来越强烈，我们从上面看到脆弱的地面爆发出一个个小洞组成的方阵。\n\n从这些洞里冒出一只只小小的石质生命。这些外表粗糙的小东西占据了视野所及的全部土地，也包括我们之前的登陆地点。与粗糙的外表相反，这些生物优雅地穿过淤泥表面，小心翼翼地不伤害脆弱的植物。值得注意的是，机载传感器仍然没有探测到任何生命迹象。\n\n它站在一起面对着地平线，似乎还没有对我们的存在作出反应。\n\n它们的行为中包含着一种期待的意味，但是在期待着什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

As the underground movement grew stronger, we watched from above as the brittle terrain erupted in a matrix of small holes.\n\nFrom each of these popped a small, lithoid lifeform. Their rough-hewn forms occupied the land from horizon to horizon, including our previous landing site. Despite their rugged exteriors, the creatures moved gracefully through the silted surface, taking care not to damage the fragile plantlife. Remarkably, onboard sensors still do not detect any signs of life.\n\nAs one, the creatures stood and faced the horizon. They have not yet reacted to our presence.\n\nThere is a sense of anticipation, but for what?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:292`

## astral_rift.2510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2510.a&lang=en)

飞向那个方向的地平线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

Fly towards the distant horizon.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:293`

## astral_rift.2515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着地下活动越来越强烈，我们从上面看到脆弱的地面爆发出一个个小洞组成的方阵。\n\n从这些洞里冒出一只只小小的石质生命。这些外表粗糙的小东西占据了视野所及的全部土地，把我们的营地包围了起来。与粗糙的外表相反，这些生物优雅地穿过淤泥表面，小心翼翼地不伤害脆弱的植物。除了有意避开我们以外，这些生物似乎对我们的存在完全没有别的任何反应。\n\n它全体一致地站起来看向地平线。其中确乎包含着一种期待的意味，但是在期待着什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

As the underground movement grew stronger, we watched as all around us the brittle terrain erupted in a matrix of small holes.\n\nFrom each of these popped a small, lithoid lifeform. Their roughly-hewn forms occupied the land from horizon to horizon, surrounding our encampment. Their coarse appearance belied their graceful movement; the creatures glided through the terrain, carefully maneuvering around our equipment and the local plant life. Besides avoiding us, none of these creatures have reacted to our presence on their planet.\n\nAll at once, the creatures stood to face the horizon. There is a definite sense of anticipation, but for what?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:297`

## astral_rift.2520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最黑暗的黎明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

Darkest Dawn

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:301`

## astral_rift.2520.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2520.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!开始顺着这群生物的目光，在行星的大气中向着地平线穿行。不久，一层薄雾开始覆盖这颗行星的灰色表面。天空中升起了一颗黑色的恒星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

The §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! began to traverse the planet's atmosphere, following the gaze of the creatures towards the horizon. Soon, a haze began to blanket the ashen surface of the planet. In the sky, a dark star was rising.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:302`

## astral_rift.2520.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2520.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

它那迷人的光辉照在我们身上，我们被它的美丽和神秘迷住了。在这壮丽的黑暗中，我们看恒星的时间越长，就有越清晰的目标感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Its mesmerizing sheen was already upon us, and as it shone we were enraptured by its beauty and mystique. In this magnificent darkness, the longer we looked at the star, the clearer our purpose became.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:303`

## astral_rift.2520.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2520.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2520.desc.common$\n\n这颗恒星的特殊辐射对我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]思维产生了异常影响。它们发生了分歧，但目前仍保持着微妙的联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

$astral_rift.2520.desc.common$\n\nThe star's peculiar radiation had an anomalous effect on the minds of our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]. They have diverged, but for now remain tenuously connected.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:304`

## astral_rift.2520.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2520.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2520.desc.common$\n\n$astral_rift.2520.desc.non_gestalt$这颗黑暗恒星的光芒让我们全身都在期待中颤抖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

$astral_rift.2520.desc.common$\n\n$astral_rift.2520.desc.non_gestalt$ The rays of this dark star sent shivers of anticipation throughout our bodies.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:305`

$astral_rift.2520.desc.common$\n\n$astral_rift.2520.desc.non_gestalt$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

$astral_rift.2520.desc.common$\n\n$astral_rift.2520.desc.non_gestalt$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:306`

### English

## astral_rift.2525.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2525.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们所观察的这个石质动物，如果把它转离日出地平线的方向，它就会激动起来，但在其他时候则完全顺从。\n\n这种生物大约有半米高，密度非常大，它的前侧是比背部浅得多的灰色。两只无色的眼睛陷在眼窝里，外表摸起来很烫。它们没有看得见的嘴巴，应该是通过其他方式获得营养的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

The lithoid creature we examined became agitated if it was turned away from the direction of the brightening horizon, but was otherwise fully compliant.\n\nThe lifeform is roughly half a meter tall and extremely dense, with its front side a much lighter shade of gray than its back. Its two bleached eyes are sunk in their sockets, and its exterior is hot to the touch. They have no visible mouths, and must receive their nourishment by some other means.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:311`

## astral_rift.2525.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2525.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2525.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

$astral_rift.2525.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:312`

## astral_rift.2525.desc.lithoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2525.desc.lithoid&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2525.desc.common$\n\n这是一种原始的生物，但具有有趣的特质和芬芳的香味。可以作为理想的宠物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

$astral_rift.2525.desc.common$\n\nA primitive species, but with interesting character and a pleasant scent. It would make for an excellent pet.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:313`

## astral_rift.2525.desc.unfriendly_nonlithoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2525.desc.unfriendly_nonlithoid&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2525.desc.common$\n\n作为实验，我们在其他同类的众目睽睽之下将样本砸成了几百块碎片，它们似乎无动于衷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

$astral_rift.2525.desc.common$\n\nAs an experiment, we hammered the specimen into hundreds of tiny pieces in full view of the others. There was no discernable reaction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:314`

## astral_rift.2525.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2525.a&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:315`

### English
