## astral_rift.2530.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2530.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最黑暗的黎明前夕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

Darkest Before the Dawn

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:318`

## astral_rift.2530.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2530.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

随着天色逐渐变黑，这些石质生物开始在原地彳亍。黑色的光撕裂开了地平线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

The lithoid creatures began to shuffle in place as the sky deepened. Black light splintered over the horizon.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:319`

## astral_rift.2530.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2530.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$\n\n一种放松感突然席卷了整个营地。我们沉浸在黑星的光芒中，感受到一种空虚，仿佛能量被虹吸走了一般。在日出前我们还有充足的时间回到§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!，但整个团队沉浸在了一种病态的好奇心中，想知道这种现象能给我们带来什么科学启示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$\n\nA sudden chill overcame our encampment. Bathed in the light of the dark star, we felt a sort of emptiness, as if our energy was being siphoned away. We have ample time to return to the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! before sunrise, but among the team there is a morbid curiosity as to what this phenomenon can offer us scientifically.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:320`

## astral_rift.2530.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2530.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$\n\n在强烈的阳光下，内部通讯链路被断开了。这个异常的天体正在从我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]中虹吸能量！初步评估仍无法确定，但这种独特的能源值得进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$\n\nInternal lines of communication dropped briefly due to a powerful solar flare. This anomalous celestial body is siphoning energy from our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]! Initial assessment is uncertain, but such a unique power source warrants further study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:321`

## astral_rift.2530.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2530.a&lang=en)

### 中文

观测黑星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Observe the dark star.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:322`

撤离此地。

### English

Retreat.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:323`

## astral_rift.2535.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2535.name&lang=en)

### 中文

黑光盲症

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Black Light Blinded

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:325`

## astral_rift.2535.desc.common.ordered_turn_away_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2535.desc.common.ordered_turn_away_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]无视了命令，继续直视着恒星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural] ignored our directive and continued to stare into the star.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:326`

## astral_rift.2535.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2535.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在无尽的光芒中直视着恒星。它的光芒照亮了我们的思想和灵魂，照亮并吞噬了它所触及的一切。黑暗之星看透了我们，温柔地带走了我们，引导我们远离无知。我们的视线被夺走了，作为回报，我们得到了目标和真理。前方的道路是清晰的，我们对知识的渴望重新焕发活力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

We gazed at the star in its infinite radiance. Its light graced our minds and our souls, illuminating and consuming everything that it touched. The Dark Star saw through us, took from us with tenderness, and shepherded us from ignorance. Our sight was taken, and in return we were granted purpose and truth. The path ahead is clear, and our thirst for knowledge revitalized.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:327`

## astral_rift.2535.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2535.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗之星传输了一条讯息。\n\n一条代码，一个信条，一个统一的原则：接受吧，[Leader.GetName]被重新编程了。结果我们的集体被调整了。这种良性干预只会支持我们向前发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

The dark star transmitted a message.\n\nA code, a creed, a unifying principle: accepting it, [Leader.GetName] was fully reprogrammed. As a result, the collective itself has been altered. This benign intervention will only support us moving forward.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:328`

## astral_rift.2535.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2535.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗之星传输了一条讯息。\n\n一条代码，一个信条，一个统一的原则：接受吧，[Leader.GetName]再也不同往昔了。结果我们的集体意识发生了变动。这种良性干预只会支持我们向前发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

The dark star transmitted a message.\n\nA code, a creed, an overwhelming thought: accepting it, [Leader.GetName] was permanently altered. As a result, the collective mind itself has been changed. This benign intervention will only support us moving forward.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:329`

## astral_rift.2535.desc.ordered_turn_away_default

## astral_rift.2540.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2540.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步观测

### English

Further Observation

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:334`

## astral_rift.2540.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2540.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们把观测设备安装到了行星的表面。仪器读数可以让我们从更安全的角度慢慢研究这颗黑星及其作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

Observation devices were installed on the planet's surface. Their readings will allow us to more safely study the dark star and its effects over time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:335`

## astral_rift.2540.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2540.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

暗星的光让人感觉近乎恶毒。即使在我们撤退的时候也能透过§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的船壳感觉到它的光线，它像一种阴险的毒药一样，把我们身体的能量吸走。\n\n$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

The light of the dark star feels almost malevolent. Even during our retreat, the touch of its rays could be felt through the hull of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!, draining energy from our bodies like an insidious poison.\n\n$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:336`

## astral_rift.2540.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2540.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

这个异常天体的光芒无比强大。它的黑暗光线甚至穿透了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!，对我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]产生了微小的内部损伤。\n\n$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

The light of this anomalous celestial body was immensely powerful. Its dark rays penetrated the hull of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!, inflicting minor internal damage to our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural].\n\n$astral_rift.2530.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:337`

## edict_automated_disinfection_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_automated_disinfection_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在一次星界裂隙之外的访问中发现的清洁无人机给了我们启示，它让我们知道了一者涉及自动机器人阵列的更有效的清洁方法。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

The cleaning drones we discovered in one of our visits beyond an Astral Rift have enlightened us on more efficient methods of sanitation involving fleets of autonomous robots.\n

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:345`

## colonization_drones_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolonization_drones_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民无人机可以识别并分类我们星球上的动植物，提供深度的生物学信息。\n\n当清理下列障碍时获取额外的£society_research£§Y$society_research$§!：\n—$d_dangerous_wildlife_blocker$\n—$d_dense_jungle$\n—$d_noxious_swamp$\n—$d_toxic_kelp$\n—$d_poisonous_algae$\n—$d_venomous_insects$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Colonization drones identify and categorize flora and fauna on our worlds, providing in-depth biological information.\n\nGain additional £society_research£ §Y$society_research$§! when clearing the following blockers:\n - $d_dangerous_wildlife_blocker$\n - $d_dense_jungle$\n - $d_noxious_swamp$\n - $d_toxic_kelp$\n - $d_poisonous_algae$\n - $d_venomous_insects$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:348`

## astral_rift.2600.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2600.name&lang=en)

### 中文

化学清洁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Chemically Cleansed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:355`

## astral_rift.2600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的大气中含有数量惊人的抗菌剂。\n\n传感器显示附近没有任何有机生命的迹象。即使在微观层面上，它也已经被彻底化学清洁了。从一个遥远的洞穴传来了微弱的生物信号，但从这个距离来讲是不可能的。\n\n我们目睹了几组机器人在空中巡逻。它们还没有对我们的存在作出反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

This planet's atmosphere contains a staggering amount of antibacterial agents.\n\nSensors indicate zero signs of organic life in the immediate vicinity. Even on a microscopic level, it has been chemically removed. There is a faint biological reading emanating from a distant cavern, but from this range it is impossible to qualify.\n\nSeveral clusters of robots have been witnessed patrolling the skies. They have not yet reacted to our presence.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:356`

## astral_rift.2600.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2600.a&lang=en)

### 中文

调查这些机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Investigate the robots.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:357`

## astral_rift.2600.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2600.b&lang=en)

### 中文

探索洞穴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

Explore the cave.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:358`

## astral_rift.2605.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2605.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Drone Encounter

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:360`

## astral_rift.2605.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2605.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们调查了一小群在空中巡逻的无人机。这些闪闪发光的金属球有一个正面的全息投影仪和一个定向推进器系统来保持它们的速度和高度。\n\n我们在接近时保持了谨慎的距离，但无人机对我们的存在作出了反应。其中四只从集群中分离出来，改变了轨道来迎接我们，但速度相当缓慢。我们还没有触发警报，但接近的无人机的能力尚不清楚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

We investigated a small group of the drones patrolling the skies. These sparkling metallic balls have a front-facing holographic projector and a system of directional thrusters to maintain their speed and altitude.\n\nWe kept a cautious distance on approach, but the drones reacted to our presence regardless. Four of them splintered from the pack and changed trajectory to meet us, albeit at a rather slow pace. We have not triggered an alarm, but the approaching drones' capabilities are unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:361`

## astral_rift.2605.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2605.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让无人机们过来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Allow the drones to approach.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:362`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2605.b&lang=en)

瘫痪它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Disable them.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:363`

## astral_rift.2610.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2610.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一丝生机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Signs of Life

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:365`

## astral_rift.2610.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2610.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

洞穴中与行星的其他任何地方都有截然不同的氛围。内部的墙壁覆盖着厚厚的太闲，土壤湿润而肥沃，充满了各种有机质。在一个隐蔽的壁龛里躺着一具干枯的鸟类遗骸。根据它的衣着和随身物品，我们认为它应该具有智能。考虑到星球的其他地方没有任何生物迹象，无论死活，所以这具尸体和周围的植物生命反而显得很异常。\n\n我们发现了一架挂在钟乳石上的无人机。虽然仍在运行，但它无法移动。这架无人机显然是为了大量洒布未知的化学溶液而设计的。这台机器的许多内部部件都完好无损，很容易维修。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

The cave has a markedly different atmosphere than the rest of the planet. The interior walls are heavy with moss and the soil is moist and rich with organic material. The desiccated remains of an avian creature lie in a secluded alcove. We assume the creature was intelligent based on its attire and belongings. Given that the rest of the planet is devoid of biological material, living or dead, this corpse and the surrounding plant life stand out as an anomaly.\n\nSkewered on a stalactite, we discovered a drone. While still operational, it is unable to move. The drone was clearly designed to distribute an unknown chemical solution in large quantities. Many of the machine's internal components remain intact and could easily be repaired.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:366`

## astral_rift.2610.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2610.a&lang=en)

### 中文

解剖鸟类尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Autopsy the avian corpse.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:367`

## astral_rift.2610.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2610.b&lang=en)

### 中文

取回无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Reactivate the drone.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:368`

## astral_rift.2615.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2615.name&lang=en)

### 中文

清洁射击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Clean Shot

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:370`

## astral_rift.2615.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2615.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

四台无人机慢慢地靠近了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!，开始进行表面检测。其中一台无人机举起了右侧的炮管准备射击。\n\n我们还没来得及反应，无人机就把压缩泡沫喷到了球体的一部分上。它的左炮管里又伸出了一个柔软而耐用的附件，开始擦洗船体上的碎屑和灰尘。其他无人机很快也效仿。\n\n随着清洁工作的进行，其中一架无人机停下来，把它的附肢换成了一根精致的棉签。随着一声响亮的嘟嘟声，它收集了一个样本，然后转身离开了。在进一步清理之后，剩下的三架无人机朝同一个方向离开了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

The four drones slowly approached the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! and examined its surface. One of the drones primed its right barrel, preparing to fire.\n\nBefore we could react, the drone squirted compressed foam over one section of the Sphere. From its left barrel, it revealed a limb with a soft-yet-durable attachment and started scrubbing away debris and dust from our hull. The other drones shortly followed suit.\n\nAs the cleaning progressed, one of the drones stopped and switched its attachment to a delicate swab. With a loud beep, it collected a sample and turned away. After some further cleaning, the three remaining drones departed in the same direction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:371`

## astral_rift.2615.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2615.a&lang=en)

### 中文

跟随无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

Follow the drones.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:372`

## astral_rift.2620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2620.name&lang=en)

### 中文

瘫痪的无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

Disabled Drones

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:374`

## astral_rift.2620.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2620.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为安全预防措施，我们向巡逻无人机附近释放了一个短程电磁脉冲。它们马上就瘫痪了，从天空中坠落到了下方贫瘠的土地上。\n\n强大的化学溶液从飞机残骸中泄漏出来，求救信号开始广播。如果我们相信我们是安全的，我们可以跟随求救信号到达预定的目的地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

As a security precaution, we released a short-range electromagnetic pulse in the vicinity of the patrolling drones. Deactivating immediately, they plummeted out of the sky, crashing onto the barren ground below.\n\nPowerful chemical solutions leaked out of their wreckage, and a distress signal began to broadcast. If we believe we are safe, we can follow the distress signal toward its intended destination.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:375`

## astral_rift.2625.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2625.name&lang=en)

### 中文

鸟类解剖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

Avian Autopsy

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:379`

## astral_rift.2625.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2625.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尸检没有发现严重的内伤或外伤。这种生物拥有鸟类的所有典型特征：中空的骨骼，角质覆盖的爪子，以及完整的羽毛。最可能的死亡原因是营养不良。\n\nDNA分析表明，这个物种的免疫系统天生就很弱。特别的一点是，它的体内还含有抗菌溶液的沉积。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

The autopsy revealed no serious internal or external wounds. The creature possessed all the typical features of an avian species: hollow bones, keratin-coated talons, and evidence of a full plumage. The most likely cause of death was malnutrition.\n\nDNA analysis indicated that this species has (or had) a naturally weak immune system. Peculiarly, the body also contained trapped deposits of antibacterial solution.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:380`

### English

## astral_rift.2630.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2630.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无人机修复

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Drone Repaired

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:385`

## astral_rift.2630.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2630.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

研究小组完成了武装准备之后，从钟乳石中救下了无人机。它利用一个紧凑的定向推进器系统，迅速开始在原地盘旋。随着一声有力的啪啪声，无人机向整个团队喷出了压缩的泡沫。从它的左桶里，露出一个柔软而耐用的附件，开始把我们擦洗干净。团队没有阻止它，因为无人机显然没有恶意。\n\n突然，无人机停了下来。它的清洁装置被一个精致的棉签取代，用来收集污垢样本。它的全息投影仪显示了一个简单的视觉界面。\n\n无人机对这些污垢进行分析和分类，作为新的清洁目标。似乎我们把一种外来物质引入了它的环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

With the research team armed and ready, we freed the drone from the stalactite. It quickly began to hover in place using a compact system of directional thrusters. With a forceful splat, the drone squirted compressed foam over the entire team. From its left barrel, it revealed a soft-yet-durable attachment and proceeded to scrub us clean. The team allowed this, as the drone clearly meant no harm.\n\nSuddenly the drone stopped. Its cleaning attachment was replaced by a delicate swab, which it used to collect a sample of dirt. Its holographic projector displayed a simple visual interface.\n\nThe dirt was analyzed and categorized by the drone as a new target for cleansing. It seems we have introduced a foreign material to its environment.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:386`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:387`

## astral_rift.2635.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2635.name&lang=en)

### 中文

造主的迹象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Signs of the Makers

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:390`

## astral_rift.2635.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2635.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们跟随着无人机的路线回到了一处先进的设施中。这栋建筑在贫瘠的景观之上高耸入云，周围环绕着无人机，每架无人机都在不同的区域之间嗡嗡作响进行着不同的任务。\n\n该设施的中心是科学设备和研究终端的连接点。奇怪的是，虽然大部分操作都是全自动的，但这个区域似乎是为人工研究而设计的；里面应该由有感知的生命来完成的任务，而不是无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

We traced the drones' route back to an advanced facility. Standing tall against the sterile landscape, the building was circled by drones, each buzzing between various zones to complete their tasks.\n\nAt the heart of the facility was a nexus of scientific equipment and research terminals. Oddly, while most of the operations were fully automated, this area seemed designed for manual research; tasks to be completed by sentient life as opposed to drones.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:391`

## astral_rift.2635.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2635.a&lang=en)

### 中文

探索实验室。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Explore the lab.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:392`

## astral_rift.2640.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2640.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超洁净假设

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

Hygienic Hypothesis

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:394`

## astral_rift.2640.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2640.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只有一个结论：这些鸟类在长期的过度消毒运动中意外地导致了自己的灭绝。作为一个天生免疫系统较弱的物种，他们创造了清洁无人机来为公共和私人空间消毒，以减少疾病的传播。\n\n然而，考虑到这颗星球的景观状况，我们可以假设清洁无人机在消灭本土生物方面做过头了，导致了星球上的有机生物多样性崩溃。简单地说，就是他们饿死了。随着尸体腐烂，无人驾驶飞机抹去了他们存在过的每一个痕迹。\n\n至于洞穴里的遗骸，由于一架功能正常的无人机在该地区的存在，其他的无人机为了提高效率都没有再把注意力放在这里，因此植物的生命才得以保持完整。这只鸟类生物利用了这种编程功能上的漏洞才得以侥幸生存。不幸的是，洞穴里贫乏的植物不能无限期地维持下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

There is only one conclusion: these avians accidentally drove themselves to extinction through a long-term campaign of over-sanitization. As a species with a naturally weak immune system, they created cleaning drones to disinfect public and private spaces in order to reduce the spread of disease.\n\nHowever, given the state of the planet's landscape, we can assume that the cleaning drones went too far in exterminating native life, resulting in the destruction of organic biodiversity on the planet. Simply put, the population starved to death. As the bodies decomposed, the drones scrubbed away every trace of their existence.\n\nAs for the remains in the cave, the presence of a functional drone in the area likely diverted others for the sake of efficiency, leaving the plant life intact. The avian creature must have exploited this programming functionality in order to survive here. Unfortunately, the cave's meager plantlife could not sustain it indefinitely.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:395`

## astral_rift.2645.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2645.name&lang=en)

### 中文

擦洗历史

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

Scrubbed History

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:400`

## astral_rift.2645.desc.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2645.desc.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

这间实验室已经很多年无人问津。与星球上的其他地方不同，这里布满了灰尘和污垢。从发光显示器收集的信息来看，无人机是完全自主的清洁设备。它们被分配到指定区域巡逻并进行消毒，在电力或化学品不足时返回设施。它们由各种可再生能源提供动力，清洗液从空气中的蒸汽和地下水中回收，创造了一个几乎可以无限运行的自我维持网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

This lab has been left untouched for years. Unlike the rest of the planet, it is caked in dust and grime. Information gleaned from the illuminated displays confirm that the drones are fully autonomous cleaning devices. They are assigned to patrol a designated area, sterilize it, and return to the facility when low on power or chemicals. Powered by a variety of renewable energy sources, their cleaning fluid is recycled from airborne vapor and groundwater, creating a self-sustaining network that can function almost indefinitely.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:401`

## astral_rift.2645.desc.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2645.desc.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推断，这些自动清洁无人机的创造者要么离开了这个星球，或者已经发生了灭绝。他们存在过的所有生物痕迹都被彻底擦洗干净了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

We theorize that the creators of the autonomous cleaning drones either left the planet or have gone extinct. Every biological trace of their existence has been scrubbed away.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:402`

## astral_rift.2645.desc

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

$astral_rift.2645.desc.common_1$\n\n$astral_rift.2645.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:403`

## astral_rift.2645.desc.clean

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2645.desc.clean&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2645.desc.common_1$\n\n对我们的船体进行过采样的那台无人机独自在实验室里盘旋，它的全息投影仪被照亮，显示一个简单的视觉界面。它从§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!中移除的物质已被分析，并归类为新的清洁目标。\n\n$astral_rift.2645.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

$astral_rift.2645.desc.common_1$\n\nThe drone that sampled our hull hovers alone in the laboratory with its holographic projector illuminated, displaying a simple visual interface. The substance it removed from the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! has already been analyzed and categorized as a new cleaning target.\n\n$astral_rift.2645.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:404`

## astral_rift.2650.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2650.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自动清洁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Automated Disinfection

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:408`

## astral_rift.2650.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2650.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在这台自动化设施上发现了各种优势和技术创新，可以为我们自己的星球带来更好的卫生和无人机管理。\n\n我们可以利用现有的优质基础设施，推行类似的清洁制度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

We noted the various strengths and technological innovations of the automated facility, with the intention of bringing improved sanitation and drone management to our own planets.\n\nWe will be able to use our existing superior infrastructure to implement a similar cleaning regime.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:409`

## astral_rift.2650.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2650.desc.default&lang=en)

### English

## astral_rift.2650.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2650.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2650.desc$\n\n此外，研究这个物种在全球范围内的努力，也能提高我们对个体思维如何能够有效合作的认识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

$astral_rift.2650.desc$\n\nFurthermore, studying planet-wide efforts by this species has improved our insight into how individual minds are able to collaborate effectively.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:411`

## astral_rift.2655.desc.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2655.desc.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能安全地关闭这座自动清洁设施，并召回它的所有无人机。一切都还保留着可以工作的状态，以便原住民返回时使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

We were able to safely shut down the automated sanitization facility and recall all of its drones. Everything was left in full working order in case of return by the native inhabitants.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:415`

## astral_rift.2655.desc.common_2

### 中文

然而，我还从设施的电力储存中收集了大量的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

However, we managed to harvest a significant amount of energy from the facility's power stores.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:416`

## astral_rift.2655.desc.unfriendly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2655.desc.unfriendly&lang=en)

### 中文

我们轻易地关闭了自动消毒设施内的系统，直到无人机网络失效。我们汲取了所有的发电机、电源存储和辅助电池，从而收集了大量的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

We haphazardly shut down systems within the automated sanitization facility until the network of drones had been disabled. We drained every last generator, power store, and ancillary battery - harvesting a significant amount of energy.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:419`

## astral_rift.2660.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2660.name&lang=en)

### 中文

干净地逃离

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

A Clean Escape

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:421`

## astral_rift.2660.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2660.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

担心有大量的这种自动无人机淹没我们，我们决定避开任何危险升级的可能性此外，这颗贫瘠的星球似乎没有提供什么研究的可能性。我们在离开时从被摧毁的无人机上收集了一些资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Faced with the risk of a huge number of these autonomous drones swarming us, we decided to avoid any chance of escalating danger. Furthermore, the sterile planet seemed to offer little in the way of research possibilities. We did manage to collect some resources from the destroyed drones as we departed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:422`

## astral_rift.2665.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2665.name&lang=en)

### 中文

改造无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Repurposing the Drone

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:424`

## astral_rift.2665.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2665.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们取回的消毒无人机可以逆向工程用于帮助我们的殖民工程。它可以用来编目当地动植物群系的细节，帮助我们的殖民者更熟悉新边境的环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

The sanitation drone we have recovered can be reverse engineered to assist in our colonization efforts. It can be repurposed to catalog the minutiae of native flora and fauna to help our colonists familiarize themselves with the terrain of new frontiers.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:425`

## astral_rift.2670.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2670.name&lang=en)

### 中文

讽刺性病毒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

Viral Irony

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:427`

## astral_rift.2670.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2670.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们编写了一个电脑病毒，并将其上载到修复后的清洁无人机的网络中。一旦病毒回到其基地就会开始传播，并最终使这个伪声基础设施瘫痪。\n\n也许这个星球最终会从化学杀菌中恢复过来，新的生命将从这个孤立的洞穴中进化出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

We engineered a potent computer virus and uploaded it to the repaired cleaning drone's network. Once it returns to its home base, the virus should spread and eventually render the sanitization infrastructure inactive.\n\nPerhaps this planet will eventually recover from its chemical sterilization and new life will evolve from this isolated cave.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:428`

## astral_rift.2675.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2675.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不要单独使用无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

Never Alone with a Drone

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:430`

## astral_rift.2675.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2675.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过一些小的调整，这架翻新的卫生无人机将成为一名出色的研究助手。它具有收集生物样本的能力，执行高灵巧的任务，以及独立飞行的能力，作为我们研究团队的一部分，它有很大的潜力。\n\n我们断开了无人机与卫生网络的连接，并安装了新的程序来补充其现有的功能。在它与[explorer.GetName]一同工作的过程中，我们将继续跟踪它的表现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

After some minor adjustments, this refurbished sanitation drone will make an excellent research assistant. With the ability to collect biological samples, perform high dexterity tasks, and the power of independent flight, it has great potential as part of our research team.\n\nWe disconnected the drone from the sanitation network and installed new features to complement its existing capabilities. We will continue to track its performance as it works alongside [explorer.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:431`

## reconverted_leader_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areconverted_leader_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一位科学官拥有了不寻常的外交手腕，把他以前的追求抛在脑后，成为一名§Y特使§!。\n\n我们将确保他们的新技能得到更好的利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

One of our scientists possesses an unusual knack for diplomacy, leaving their previous pursuits behind to become an §YEnvoy§!.\n\nWe will make sure their new-found skills are put to better use.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:440`

## forceful_winds_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aforceful_winds_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的大气中住满了感性的风形生物，与我们和谐共存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

The atmosphere of this world is populated by sentient wind creatures that live alongside us in harmony.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:449`

## astral_rift.2700.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2700.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风扫行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

Windswept Planet

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:451`

## astral_rift.2700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们来到了一个被猛烈的狂风摧残着的星球。它的表面几乎是光秃秃的，悬崖和巨石被风化得很光滑。\n\n乌云密布，天空一片漆黑，当闪电击中我们周围的地面时，明亮的气团猛烈地喷发出来。\n\n我们应该尽快寻找避风的地方躲避即将来临的暴风雨。气象传感器显示，平流层高处的气流比较温和，我们可以在那里躲避危险的泪点。或者，附近有一个大洞穴也可以提供避难所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

We have arrived on a planet battered by fierce, high-velocity winds. The surface is nearly bare, with its cliffs and boulders weathered smooth by the elements.\n\nThe skies above are dark with thunderous clouds and plumes of bright gas erupt violently as lightning strikes the ground around us.\n\nWe should soon seek shelter from the gathering storm. Meteorological sensors indicate gentler air currents in the higher stratosphere where we could avoid the dangerous electrical discharges. Alternatively, there is a large cavern nearby that could provide refuge.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:452`

## astral_rift.2700.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2700.a&lang=en)

### 中文

升到暴风之上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

Rise above the storm.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:453`

## astral_rift.2700.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2700.b&lang=en)

### 中文

在下方的洞穴避险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Shelter in the cave below.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:454`

## astral_rift.2705.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2705.name&lang=en)

### 中文

平流层之上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Beyond the Stratosphere

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:456`

## astral_rift.2705.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2705.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

突破云层，我们看见了湛蓝的天空。风继续拍打着我们的船身，每一阵风都更猛烈些。\n\n这些气流不仅仅是一种特殊的天气模式，它们的行为也异常精确。更重要的是，我们在旋转的空气中发现了一种类似激素的化合物。随后的分析显示，这与植物受到攻击时的生物反应惊人地相似。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Breaking through the cloud layer, we observed a piercing blue sky. Wind continued to whip against our hull, gaining strength with every gust.\n\nMore than just a peculiar weather pattern, these currents act with unnatural precision. What's more, we detected a hormone-like compound in the swirling air. Subsequent analysis revealed it is strikingly similar to a biological response from a plant under attack.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:457`

## astral_rift.2705.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2705.a&lang=en)

### 中文

对风进行取样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Take a sample of the wind.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:458`

## astral_rift.2710.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2710.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌洞穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Fungal Cavern

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:460`

## astral_rift.2710.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2710.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们顺着无情的狂风往下走，穿过厚厚的含硫的孢子云，进入了这个可以遮蔽天气的洞穴。\n\n穿过狭窄的通道后，我们进入了一个空间很高大的洞窟。厚厚的钟乳石把浑浊的液体滴在黄疸的土壤上，土壤里充满了黄色的真菌。在一簇簇蘑菇之间，成群的昆虫在潮湿的地面上轻快地飞舞，昏暗处生长着苍白的植被。陡峭的墙壁上长满了苔藓，浑浊的空气中弥漫着一层厚厚的孢子雾。\n\n在洞里，我们探测到一个微弱的电场：一个由冲动和突触组成的网络，它似乎连接着各种真菌群。也许我们可以进入这个网络，与蘑菇交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Descending through the unrelenting torrent of wind, we maneuvered through thick, sulfurous clouds of spores until we entered a weather-shielded cave.\n\nAfter navigating through tight passages, we entered a huge chamber with a high ceiling. Thick stalactites dripped cloudy liquid onto jaundiced soil which was replete with a yellow fungus. Between the clusters of mushrooms, colonies of insects skittered briskly along the damp terrain and pale vegetation grew in the gloom. Moss coated the steep walls, and a thick haze of spores hung in the stagnant air.\n\nWithin the cave, we detected a weak field of electricity; a network of impulses and synapses that seemingly connects the various fungal clusters. Perhaps we can tap into this network and communicate with the mushrooms.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:461`

## astral_rift.2710.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2710.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Attempt to communicate.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:462`

## astral_rift.2715.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2715.name&lang=en)

### 中文

活着的风

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Living Wind

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:464`

## astral_rift.2715.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2715.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的取样表明，在风中存在着微观组织。\n\n作为实验，我们合成了相似的信息素，将其释放到周围的大气中。突然，狂暴的风转变成了温和的气流。\n\n对样品进一步的研究表明，在足够大的簇中，这些微观组织可以构成一个独立的意识。它们互相合作，可以在风中产生“有生命”的器官。\n\n有一些气流中携带者像发丝一般的基因物质。在同样的气流中，我们推回地面，那里的风暴已经平息了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Our sample confirmed the presence of microorganisms within the wind.\n\nAs an experiment, we synthesized a similar hormone and released it into the surrounding atmosphere. Suddenly, the brutal winds transformed into a much gentler current.\n\nFurther analysis of the sample indicated that, in large enough clusters, these microorganisms comprise a single mind. Working together, they are able to generate "living" gusts of wind.\n\nSome of these currents carry hair-like strands of genetic material. Caught in the same current, we were pushed back toward the surface where the storm had now subsided.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:465`

## astral_rift.2715.a

## astral_rift.2720.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2720.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌之中

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Among the Fungus

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:468`

## astral_rift.2720.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2720.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们利用神经网络的菌丝突触，发出一个短脉冲；每个菌盖都突然绷紧了，显然是被他们内部网络上的一个新角色弄糊涂了。作为回应，我们收到了大量的信号，最终破译了真菌的沟通方式。\n\n这种有机体是一种蜂群思维，将自己视为星球本土物种的守护者。在地表之下，各种各样的动植物都被菌丝团保存和保护着。作为交换，真菌将它们的废物作为食物。\n\n然而，有东西在威胁着这种共生关系和真菌本身的持续生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Tapping into the network's mycelial synapses, we issued a short current; every mushroom cap stiffened suddenly, obviously bewildered by a new actor on their internal network. We received a frenzy of signals in response, but eventually we managed to decipher the fungus' transmission.\n\nThe organism is a hive mind that views itself as the custodian of the planet's native species. Beneath the surface, all manner of flora and fauna are preserved and protected by the mycelial clusters. In exchange, the fungus consumes their waste as sustenance.\n\nHowever, a threat exists to this symbiotic relationship, and to the continued survival of the fungus itself.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:469`

## astral_rift.2720.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2720.a&lang=en)

### 中文

保持真菌通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Maintain fungal correspondence.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:470`

## astral_rift.2725.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2725.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌威胁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Fungal Threat

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:472`

## astral_rift.2725.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2725.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们到达了行星的表面，其上漂浮着一层厚厚的云雾。尽管地景几乎一马平川寸草不生，但每一个洞穴和裂隙中都栖息着一些散发着孢子的真菌簇。\n\n我们跟随的气流在这里拂过大地，释放出一捆捆毛茸茸的气生的卵。这些卵飘向地面，大部分很快被真菌孢子的薄毯分解。\n\n存活下来的少量菌种附着在光秃秃的地形上，形成一个圆锥形的水螅体，为风传微生物的安全成熟提供了一个受保护的环境。\n\n显然，真菌孢子阻止了风生物的繁殖。反过来，风生生物夷平了星球表面，迫使真菌撤退到最隐蔽的环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

We reached the planet's surface, over which hovered a thick layer of yellow clouds. While the landscape was level and mostly bare, every hidden cavity and crevice harbored clusters of spore-spewing fungi.\n\nThe air currents we followed here flowed over the landscape, releasing bundles of hairy, air borne spawn. These floated down towards the surface, where most were quickly disintegrated by the blanket of fungal spores.\n\nWhat little spawn survived attached itself to the bare terrain and formed a conical polyp, providing a protected environment for the wind borne microorganisms to safely mature.\n\nClearly, the fungal spores are preventing the wind creatures from reproducing. In turn, the wind creatures have leveled the planet's surface, forcing the fungus to retreat to the most sheltered environments.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:473`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:474`

### English

## astral_rift.2730.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2730.name&lang=en)

### 中文

食肉之风

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Carnivorous Winds

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:477`

## astral_rift.2730.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2730.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这群真菌把我们带回到洞穴入口，那里的风暴已经平息了。一个小小的爬虫类动物在门槛前犹豫了一下，然后进入了开阔的荒原。几乎与此同时，一股猛烈的风把这只爬行动物卷到了空中。\n\n我们什么也做不了，只能看着它被切成越来越小的碎片，然后被空气中的酶消化。\n\n在真菌构成的避难所之外，地表上的生命完全受食肉风的支配。考虑到它的精准度和反应能力，我们可以有把握地认为，风至少在某种程度上是有知觉的。\n\n而且饥肠辘辘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

The fungus guided us back to the cave entrance, where the storm had subsided. A small, reptilian creature hesitated at the threshold before crossing into the open wastes. Almost immediately, a sharp current of wind scooped the reptile into the air.\n\nWe could do nothing but watch as it was sliced into smaller and smaller pieces, only to be digested by enzymes in the air.\n\nOutside of the sanctuary maintained by the fungus, life on the surface is at the mercy of the carnivorous wind. Given its brutal accuracy and responsiveness, it is safe to assume the wind is at least somewhat sentient.\n\nAnd hungry.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:478`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2735.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Fungal Transmission

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:482`

## astral_rift.2735.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2735.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沿着行星表面行驶，我们接近了一群散发孢子的真菌。组织样本显示，这是一种高度复杂的蜂群思维。通过在节点之间传输快速的电脉冲，进行复杂的网络通信。\n\n我们利用神经网络的菌丝突触发出一个短电流；每个菌盖都突然绷紧了，显然是被他们内部网络上的一个新角色弄糊涂了。作为回应，我们收到了大量信号，其含义尚不清楚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Traveling along the surface of the planet, we approached a cluster of the spore-emitting fungus. A tissue sample revealed that it is a highly complex hive mind. By transmitting rapid electrical impulses between nodes, it communicates across an intricate network.\n\nTapping into the network's mycelial synapses, we issued a short current; every mushroom cap stiffened suddenly, obviously bewildered by a new actor on their internal network. We received a frenzy of signals in response, the meaning of which remains unclear.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:483`

## astral_rift.2735.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2735.a&lang=en)

### 中文

解码真菌通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Decipher the fungal transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:484`

## astral_rift.2740.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2740.name&lang=en)

### 中文

当前事件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Current Events

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:486`

## astral_rift.2740.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2740.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在离开洞穴以后以后，我们测量了船周围的强风。我们在周围的空气中发现了一种类似激素的化合物，并对其进行了分析。\n\n这些激素与某些受到威胁的植物释放的激素相似。我们还证实了一种微生物的存在，可能就是它在控制着风。作为实验，我们合成了一种类似的激素并将其释放到大气中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

After exiting the cave, we measured the strong gusts of wind circling our vessel. We detected a hormone-like compound in the surrounding air and analyzed it.\n\nThe hormones are similar to those released by certain plants under threat. We have also confirmed the presence of a species of microorganism, which we hypothesize is controlling the wind. As an experiment, we synthesized a similar hormone and released it into the atmosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:487`

### English

## astral_rift.2745.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2745.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌之辩护

### English

The Case for the Fungus

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:490`

## astral_rift.2745.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2745.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在解密了真菌的通讯后，我们发现，这种风形生物正在摧毁行星的生态系统。这些活着的风扫过地表寻找食物，它灭绝了这些真菌赖以生存的生物多样性。\n\n这些真菌带我们到了一处地下洞穴，数百种植物、动物和昆虫被它们保护起来，免受风的侵蚀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

After deciphering the fungus' transmission, it is now clear that the wind creatures are destroying the planet's ecosystem. As the living wind scours the surface for food, it eradicates the biodiversity the fungus relies on to survive.\n\nThe fungus guided us to an underground cave where hundreds of species of plants, animals, and insects have been sheltered from the forceful winds.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:491`

## astral_rift.2745.desc.common.friendly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2745.desc.common.friendly&lang=en)

### 中文

争斗的双方陷入了僵局，但我们有能力解决其中的一边。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

The two sides in this struggle are at an impasse - one we have the ability to overcome.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:492`

## astral_rift.2745.desc.common.unfriendly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2745.desc.common.unfriendly&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:493`

## astral_rift.2750.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2750.name&lang=en)

### 中文

狂风之辩护

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

The Case for the Wind

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:501`

## astral_rift.2750.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2750.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们释放信息素后，狂风很快就平息了。\n\n一阵微风吹过我们的船体，把一捆毛茸茸的气生卵撒在空中。这些卵浮向地面，大部分很快被真菌孢子的覆盖分解。\n\n当幸存的卵到达地面时，它附着在裸露的地形上，形成一个圆锥形的息肉，为风传微生物的安全成熟提供了一个受保护的环境。\n\n我们的结论是，风展示了真菌是如何试图阻止它们的繁殖过程的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

The harsh winds subsided soon after we released the hormone.\n\nA light breeze passed over our vessel, releasing a bundle of hairy, airborne spawn. These floated down towards the surface, where most were quickly disintegrated by the blanket of fungal spores.\n\nWhen the surviving spawn did reach the ground, it attached itself to the bare terrain and formed a conical polyp, providing a protected environment for the wind borne microorganisms to safely mature.\n\nWe concluded that the wind was demonstrating how the fungus is attempting to thwart their reproductive processes.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:502`

## astral_rift.2755.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2755.name&lang=en)

### 中文

镰鼬之风

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Cutting Winds

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:510`

## astral_rift.2755.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2755.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为风中生物提供了大量的合金碎屑。这些磨料很轻，可以被单独的气流 冲走，但又足够强大，可以在短时间内磨掉大多数矿物质。\n\n一股气流迅速利用这些碎屑撕碎了躲在附近裂缝里的一簇黄色真菌。\n\n风中生物继续将金属屑更广泛地分布在它们之间。猛烈的风开始袭击行星表面，在一场协调一致的轰炸中瞄准了喷出孢子的真菌。\n\n也许是为了表示感谢，一阵微风给我们送来了一捆毛茸茸的浮卵。有了这些，我们就可以培育自己的“活风”，把高速气流带到我们选定的一些殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

We provided the wind creatures with a large quantity of alloyed metal filings. These abrasives were light enough to be swept along individual currents but strong enough to wear away most minerals over a short time.\n\nA group of air currents quickly used these filings to shred a cluster of yellow fungus sheltering inside a nearby crevice.\n\nThe wind creatures went on to distribute the metal filings more broadly among themselves. Sharp winds began to strike at the planet's surface, targeting the spore-spewing fungus in a coordinated bombardment.\n\nIn what was perhaps a gesture of gratitude, a gentle breeze delivered us a bundle of its hairy, floating spawn. With these, we can foster our own "living winds", bringing high-velocity air currents to a select group of our colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:511`

## astral_rift.2760.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2760.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌绽放

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Fungal Bloom

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:514`

## astral_rift.2760.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2760.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在星球表面散布了大量的矿物质营养物质，并在重新焕发活力的土壤中引入了新的抗风植物。然后，真菌能够在新肥沃的土地上传播大量的孢子。\n\n现有的真菌群在规模和复原力上已经在增长。随着时间的推移，真菌会沿着行星的整个表面蔓延，使风的传播失去动力。\n\n它的节点充满了新发现的力量，真菌沿着它的菌丝网络传递着感激的信息。\n\n一种以前未被发现的真菌标本随后从我们附近的地面发芽。它的球茎腔充满了高浓度的外来气体。我们相信，培育这些“泡沫球”将有助于我们生产这种宝贵的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

We distributed a large quantity of mineral nutrients across the planet's surface, and introduced new wind-resistant plant life to the reinvigorated soil. The fungus was then able to spread a huge quantity of spores on the newly-fertile land.\n\nExisting fungal clusters are already growing in size and resilience. Over time, the fungus should creep along the entire surface of the planet, starving-out the propagations of the wind.\n\nIts nodes bursting with a new-found strength, the fungus transmitted a message of gratitude along its mycelial network.\n\nA previously undiscovered fungal specimen then sprouted from the ground near our location. Its bulbous cavities were inflated with high concentrations of exotic gases. We are confident that cultivating these "puffballs" will aid us in the production of this valuable resource.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:515`

## astral_rift.2765.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2765.name&lang=en)

### 中文

困难的协商

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

A Difficult Compromise

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:518`

## astral_rift.2765.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2765.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌和风中生物之间交流的极端差异导致了一场艰苦而复杂的讨论。然而，我们已经在这两个物种之间达成了协议。\n\n只要它们不干扰现有的孢子群，风生物将有权在星球表面的指定区域狩猎和繁殖。与此同时，真菌的蜂群思维被赋予了同等的空间，在那里它们可以毫无畏惧地传播孢子，在它们的保护网络下容纳无数其他物种。\n\n在这种安排下，这个星球独特的生态系统有了新的繁荣机会。我们已经安装了观测卫星，从裂隙外监视它的进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

The extreme differences in communication between the fungus and the wind creatures resulted in an arduous, complex discussion. However, we have brokered an agreement between the two species.\n\nSo long as they do not disturb existing spore clusters, the wind creatures will have the right to hunt and breed on designated portions of the planet's surface. Meanwhile, the fungal hive mind has been granted an equal part of the landscape where they can spread their spores without fear, hosting the myriad of other species beneath their protective network.\n\nUnder this arrangement, the planet's unique ecosystem has a new opportunity to prosper. We have installed observation satellites to keep tabs on its progress from beyond the rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:519`

## astral_rift.2770.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2770.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生物灭绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

Biological Eradication

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:522`

## astral_rift.2770.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2770.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

两个[Explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种之间的斗争实在是烦人。\n\n我们合成了一种强效酸剂，并将其雾化到行星大气层中。随着腐蚀剂的扩散，风生物溶解成灰尘，真菌枯萎变黑。我们能够利用星球大气中现有的化合物，将这种效应级联到整个世界。\n\n在确认这颗行星已经完全被安乐死后，我们把所有有用的物质都拣了出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

This quarrel between these [Explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species has become tiresome.\n\nWe synthesized a potent acid and aerosolized it within the planet's atmosphere. As the caustic agent spread, the wind creatures dissolved into dust and the fungus shriveled and blackened. We were able to exploit existing compounds within the planet's atmosphere to cascade this effect across the entire world.\n\nAfter confirming that the planet had been fully euthanized, we picked it clean of all useful material.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:523`

## astral_rift.2790.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2790.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.GetName]最近在我们所研究的[from.solar_system.GetName]裂隙另一端达成了一项复杂的协议，[this.GetHasHave]请求调换职务。在经历了如此艰难的仲裁过程后，[this.GetHeShe]对谈判产生了新的热情。如果我们允许，[this.GetHeShe]将离开科学界，成为我们现有外交基础设施的常驻使臣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

[this.GetName], who recently brokered a complex agreement on the other side of the rift we were studying in [from.solar_system.GetName], [this.GetHasHave] requested a change of duty. After navigating such a difficult arbitration process, [this.GetHeShe] [this.GetHasHave] discovered a new-found passion for negotiation. If we allow it, [this.GetHeShe] would leave the world of science behind to become a permanent envoy in our existing diplomatic infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:528`

## astral_rift.2800.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2800.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小小星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

It's a Small World

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:547`

## astral_rift.2800.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2800.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们到达了一颗引力特性十分奇特的恒星系。与我们的维度相比，在这个空间中单个物体的引力相对于它们的大小要强得多。\n\n传感器检测到该恒星系中有四颗非常小的天体，其中一个还是个[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]社会的居住地。这是一个由半水生两栖物种居住的陆地星球，除了整个行星在尺寸上大致与我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!大小相同之外，它完全没有什么不寻常的地方！\n\n这个微小的智慧物种和他们的家园提供了各种潜在的研究机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

We have arrived in a star system with peculiar gravitational properties. Individual bodies traveling through this space have a much stronger gravitational pull relative to their size than in our dimension.\n\nSensors detect four extremely small celestial bodies in this system, one of which is populated by a [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] society. A continental world inhabited by a semi-aquatic amphibian species, it is wholly typical aside from the fact that the entire planet is roughly the size of our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nThis extremely small intelligent species and their homeworld offer a variety of potential research opportunities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:548`

## astral_rift.2800.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2800.a&lang=en)

### English

## astral_rift.2805.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2805.name&lang=en)

### 中文

月面冲击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

Lunar Impact

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:551`

## astral_rift.2805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于这颗行星与我们的飞船大小相仿，我们靠近的时候非常小心以免发生碰撞，注意我们也可能因为引力作用影响到它。\n\n然而，我们没有准确地把它的卫星的轨道考虑在内。不一会儿，一次碰撞就发生了，卫星被抛向了更远的太空。\n\n考虑到天然卫星对行星生态系统的效应，我们预计[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种会遭受到严重的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

Given that the tiny planet itself is a similar size to our vessel, we were careful not to approach it too closely, concerned we might affect it with our gravitational pull.\n\nHowever, we did not account properly for the trajectory of the planet's moon. Within moments, a collision occurred and the moon was flung further into space.\n\nGiven the effect a natural satellite can have on a planet's ecosystem, we expect severe repercussions for the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:552`

## astral_rift.2805.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2805.a&lang=en)

### 中文

代价惨痛的误算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

A costly miscalculation.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:553`

## astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2810.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗袖珍行星上的[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种注意到了我们的存在。由于我们的舰船相对大小，想必是占据了他们天空的很大一块区域。\n\n根据当地的无线电信号，他们也发现了卫星的轨道被往外移动了，并导致了大范围的痛苦。危险的巨浪导致这个[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种的大量伤亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

The [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species on the tiny planet has noticed our presence. Given the relative size of our vessel, we must be dominating the view from most of their horizons.\n\nAccording to localized radio signals, they have also noticed their moon's orbit has shifted outward, causing a widespread panic. Dangerous tidal surges have resulted in significant loss of life among the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:556`

## astral_rift.2810.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2810.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还是可以继续远远地观测他们，避免进一步干涉他们的文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

Otherwise, we can continue to observe from afar so as not to disrupt their civilization even more.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:557`

## astral_rift.2810.desc.common.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2810.desc.common.3&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还是可以继续远远地观测摧毁情况，记录这场影响他们子孙后代的灾难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Otherwise, we can continue to observe the destruction from afar and record the disaster for posterity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:558`

## astral_rift.2810.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2810.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1$\n\n如果认为合适，我们还可以尝试与社会沟通。\n\n$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1$\n\nIf deemed appropriate, we can attempt to communicate with this society.\n\n$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:559`

## astral_rift.2810.desc.barbaric_despoilers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2810.desc.barbaric_despoilers&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1$\n\n与这个社会建立通讯或许还有机会讹点赎金。\n\n$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1$\n\nEstablishing communications with this society may provide an opportunity to demand ransom.\n\n$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:560`

## astral_rift.2810.desc.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2810.desc.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1$\n\n我们有能力截获他们的通讯，然后对他们想说什么就说什么。\n\n$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.1$\n\nWe are capable of intercepting their communications and sending whatever communication we wish.\n\n$astral_rift.2810.desc.common.3$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:561`

告诉他们末日要来咯。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.name&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯建立。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Communications Established

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:568`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在利用我们有限的翻译能力与该物种进行了一次[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]不成熟的对话后，我们知道它们的领导层对我们的存在同样感到恐惧和震惊。我们似乎不受欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

After a stunted conversation with the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species using our limited translation capacity, we know that their leadership is equally terrified and astounded by our presence. We do not appear to be welcome.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:569`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

语言障碍阻碍了复杂的外交道路，但我们应该选择适当的行动方针。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

The language barrier prevents a complex diplomatic path moving forward, but we should choose an appropriate course of action.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:570`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\n这些外星人的胆子也……也许我们应该做点什么来证明他们的恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\nThe gall of these aliens... Perhaps we should do something to justify their fear.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:571`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\n尽管他们不清楚我们是当前情况的始作俑者，但我们总有办法可以弥补自己的过失。\n\n$astral_rift.2815.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\nThough they are not aware we are the cause of their current predicament, there must be something we can do to make amends for our mistake.\n\n$astral_rift.2815.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:572`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\n$astral_rift.2815.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\n$astral_rift.2815.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:573`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.barbaric_despoilers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.barbaric_despoilers&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\n我们的索要得到了他们敌意的回应，而且看起来他们也没什么值钱的东西能给我们嘛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

$astral_rift.2815.desc.common$\n\nOur demands for ransom were met with hostility, and they seem to have nothing of value to offer.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:574`

## astral_rift.2815.desc.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.desc.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们利用有限的翻译能力为这些[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种制作了一条信息，并在它们所有的交流渠道上传播。\n\n§E“见证自己的末日吧。你所知和已知的一切很快就会消失在时间的长河里。未来将没有你们的存在。我们将带给你们遗忘。”§!\n\n他们没有回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

We crafted a message for the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species using our limited translation capacity, and broadcast it across all their channels of communication.\n\n§E"Look upon your end. All that you know and have known will soon be lost to time. There is no future in which you continue to exist. We bring you oblivion."§!\n\nThere was no response.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:575`

## astral_rift.2815.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.a&lang=en)

试着修复卫星轨道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

Attempt to fix the moon's orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:576`

摧毁卫星。

### English

Destroy the moon.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:577`

切断通讯。被动观测。

Cut communications. Observe passively.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2815.d&lang=en)

切断通讯。静观灾难扩散。
