## astral_rift.2820.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2820.name&lang=en)

### 中文

回到轨道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

Back in Orbit

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:581`

## astral_rift.2820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过了许多计算、模拟和测试后，我们成功地将这颗袖珍行星的卫星推回到原来的轨道上。这不会消除潮汐变化已经造成的破坏和生命损失，但我们已经尽了最大努力。\n\n与[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]文明领袖的通讯频道打开了。他们似乎对我们的努力颇为感谢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

After many calculations, simulations, and trials, we managed to delicately nudge the small planet's moon back on its original orbital trajectory. This will not undo the damage and loss of life the tidal shifts have already caused, but we have done the best we can.\n\nCommunications with the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] leaders are now open. They seem grateful for our efforts.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:582`

## astral_rift.2825.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2825.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永别卫星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

No More Moon

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:584`

## astral_rift.2825.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2825.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

利用摆在我们面前的独特的研究机会，我们摧毁了绕着这颗袖珍行星运行的卫星。我们轻易地把它放在回收臂的颚间夹碎，它的细尘逐渐在行星周围形成了一个薄薄的环。\n\n这在两极之间造成了各种极端天气状况。从那以后，这个星球上的两栖动物一直疯狂地试图与我们交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Taking advantage of the unique research opportunity presented to us, we destroyed the moon orbiting this tiny world. It was easy enough to crush between the jaws of our retrieval arm, and we observed its fine dust gradually form a thin ring around the planet.\n\nThis caused a variety of extreme weather conditions from pole to pole. The planet's amphibian inhabitants have since been frantically attempting to communicate with us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:585`

## astral_rift.2825.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2825.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2825.desc.common$\n\n现在看来把它们直接灭绝了会更加仁慈一些；这么做甚至仅仅只需要用我们的裂隙探球施加很小的一点撞击力就行。然而，我们还是有进一步研究的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

$astral_rift.2825.desc.common$\n\nIt may be the most merciful option to extinguish them entirely; it would take no more than a small amount of blunt force from our Rift Sphere. However, further research opportunities do remain.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:586`

## astral_rift.2825.desc.unfriendly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2825.desc.unfriendly&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2825.desc.common$\n\n他们无助地看着狂风扫过整个行星的表面。他们命数已定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

$astral_rift.2825.desc.common$\n\nThey are helpless as storms rage across their planet. Their fate is sealed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:587`

## astral_rift.2825.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2825.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们脱离苦海吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

Put them out of their misery.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:588`

## astral_rift.2825.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2825.b&lang=en)

### 中文

部署遥测研究设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

Deploy remote research devices.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:589`

## astral_rift.2830.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2830.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远距离观测

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

Observed From Afar

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:591`

## astral_rift.2830.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2830.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在微型恒星系统中放置了监视设备，继续研究这颗微小的行星和[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]物种的后续发展情况。我们很好奇卫星碰撞对环境的影响，以及这个物种是否能在事态变得太严重之前找到一种方法来抵消它的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

We placed surveillance equipment within the miniature star system to continue studying this tiny planet and the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] species as the situation unfolds. We are curious to see the environmental impacts of our lunar collision, and whether the species can develop a way to counteract its effects before they become too severe.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:592`

## astral_rift.2830.desc.communications

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2830.desc.communications&lang=en)

### 中文

我们切断了与[explorer.GetPreFTLLower]领袖的通讯，恢复了被动观测。\n\n$astral_rift.2830.desc.default$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

We severed communications with the [explorer.GetPreFTLLower] leaders and resumed passive observations.\n\n$astral_rift.2830.desc.default$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:593`

## astral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly.common&lang=en)

### 中文

无论如何，继续看着这种摧毁的演进是一种有趣的娱乐活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

In any case, watching the destruction continue will provide some entertainment.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:594`

## astral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2830.desc.default$\n\n$astral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

$astral_rift.2830.desc.default$\n\n$astral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:595`

## astral_rift.2830.desc.unfriendly.communications

## astral_rift.2835.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2835.name&lang=en)

### 中文

突破潮汐锁定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

Tidal Locking Breakthrough

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:598`

## astral_rift.2835.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2835.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

给予我们对这颗行星的卫星进行的实验性操作，我们加深了对绕天体运行的卫星一般行为的理解。我们现在可以运用这些知识来更精确地调整我们自己星系内的行星。\n\n我们下面的袖珍星球则正在为我们举行庆祝活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

As a result of our experimental manipulations with this planet's moon, we have deepened our understanding on the general behavior of satellites orbiting celestial bodies. We can now apply this knowledge to more precisely align planets within our own systems.\n\nThe tiny planet below us is currently holding celebrations in our honor.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:599`

## astral_rift.2835.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2835.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

你现在可以用行星决议将任意拥有卫星行星进行§Y潮汐锁定§!\n§Y昼半球太阳能农场§!将有双倍的产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

You may now §YTidal Lock§! any planet with a moon as a planetary decision.\n§YDayside Solar Farms§! are twice as effective.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:600`

## astral_rift.2840.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2840.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浅冲击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

Shallow Impact

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:602`

## astral_rift.2840.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2840.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最大限度地增强了我们的护盾，将推进器的能量调至最大，准备好与这个微小行星碰撞。\n\n在碰撞的瞬间，我们压缩了这颗陆地星球的大气层，并在全球产生了极高压力的冲击波。由此产生的摩擦使空气超加热，蒸发了行星的海洋，并灭绝了所有生命。\n\n这个曾经繁荣的星球所剩下的东西，将成为我们可供展示的一件令人心生畏惧的小玩意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

We maximized our shields, charged our thrusters, and prepared for impact with the tiny planet.\n\nAt the moment of impact, we compressed the continental world's atmosphere and sent a shockwave of extreme pressure around the globe. The resulting friction superheated the air, which vaporized the planet's oceans and exterminated all life.\n\nWhat remains of this once-flourishing world will make for an intimidating trinket to for us to place on display.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:603`

## astral_rift.2840.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2840.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

你可以使用行星决议展示微行星的地壳。只能这么做一次。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

You can display the Microplanet Husk as a planetary decision. It can be done only once.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:604`

## astral_rift.2845.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2845.name&lang=en)

### 中文

模型行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Model Planet

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:606`

## astral_rift.2845.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2845.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

观测设备已被部署，以继续研究这颗袖珍行星上的气候条件。\n\n猛烈的暴风雪、干旱、暴雨和各种极端天气已经开始肆虐这个小小的世界。我们在这里的实验将继续提供独特的气象和人类学数据，直到本地物种不可避免地灭绝为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Observation equipment has been deployed to continue studying climatic conditions on the tiny planet.\n\nBlistering snowstorms, droughts, torrential rain, and all manner of extreme weather has begun to ravage the little world. Our experiments here will continue to provide unique meteorological and anthropological data until the native species is inevitably wiped out.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:607`

## astral_rift.2890.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2890.name&lang=en)

### 中文

袖珍行星大灭绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Tiny Planet Extinction

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:609`

## astral_rift.2890.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2890.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[from.solar_system.GetName]星界裂隙另一端的这颗微型行星被剧烈的气候变化摧毁了。我们手机的研究数据到此为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

The minute planet on the far side of the Astral Rift in [from.solar_system.GetName] has been decimated by extreme shifts in climate. The research data we were collecting there has ceased.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:610`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2890.a&lang=en)

## edict_spontaneous_crystallization_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_spontaneous_crystallization_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过向新收获的岩石施加特定的催化剂组合，我们有一定概率可以产生更有价值的晶体结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

By applying a unique matrix of catalysts onto freshly harvested rock, we have a chance at generating much more valuable crystalline structures.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:620`

## edict_spontaneous_crystallization_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_spontaneous_crystallization_tooltip&lang=en)

将至多1%的£minerals£矿物收入转变为£rare_crystals£稀有水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

Convert up to 1% of total £minerals£ Mineral income into £rare_crystals£ Rare Crystals.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:621`

## astral_rift.2900.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2900.name&lang=en)

### 中文

走过路过不要错过

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Come One, Come All

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:623`

## astral_rift.2900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们到达了一个被无数虫洞包围着的熙熙攘攘的星系。各种形状和大小的宇宙飞船围绕着中央明亮的空间站。\n\n这座巨大的建筑上覆盖着五颜六色的标志，上面写着花哨而陌生的文字。这似乎是一座娱乐设施，旨在吸引观众观看一场盛大的表演。考虑到参展船只的数量，无论在这里举行什么展览，它都吸引了令人印象深刻的人群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

We have arrived in a bustling star system surrounded by numerous wormholes. Voidcraft of various shapes and sizes circle a central, brightly lighted space station.\n\nThe enormous structure is covered with multicolored signs in garish, unfamiliar script. It seems like an entertainment facility designed to attract an audience for a grand spectacle. Given the number of vessels present, whatever show is taking place here, it has drawn an impressive crowd.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:624`

## astral_rift.2900.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2900.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

Continue investigations.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:625`

## astral_rift.2905.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2905.name&lang=en)

### 中文

参与其中

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Anticipation

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:627`

## astral_rift.2905.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2905.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们安全地停泊在了其中一个繁忙的太空站上。刚一进去，我们就一边加入人群一边继续着调查行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

We docked at the busy space station without incident. Once inside, we continued our investigation as part of the crowd.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:628`

## astral_rift.2905.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2905.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

在里面，我们看到了一座巨大的圆形剧场。熙熙攘攘的室内挤满了各种感性外星生命，他们纷纷注视着一个中央表演区域。演出马上就要开始了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Inside, we beheld an amphitheater of epic proportions. The bustling interior was crowded with a variety of sentient alien lifeforms, each focused on a central performance area. The show will soon begin.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:629`

## astral_rift.2905.organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2905.organic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们接近中央穹顶的大门时，一位机器人服务员让我们在和其他顾客一起通过之前，擦拭了我们每个人的[explorer.species.GetHandNamePlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

As we approached the gates of the central dome, a robotic attendant swabbed each of our [explorer.species.GetHandNamePlural] before allowing us to pass with the other patrons.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:630`

## astral_rift.2905.inorganic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2905.inorganic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们接近中央穹顶的大门时，一位机器人服务员用一台小装置简单地扫描了我们每个人，然后才让我们和其他客户一同通过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

As we approached the gates of the central dome, a robotic attendant briefly scanned each of us with a small device before allowing us to pass with the other patrons.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:631`

### English

Very well.

## astral_rift.2910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2910.name&lang=en)

### 中文

跃动的狐狸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

The Vaulting Vulpines

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:636`

## astral_rift.2910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们刚落座，舞台上就出现了一群穿着闪闪发光服装的狐类哺乳动物。在短暂的高级体操表演之后，舞台中央出现了一个小虫洞。就在洞消失之前，一个勇敢的杂技演员跳进了洞里。另一个虫洞出现在人群上方，把表演者甩了出来，他灵巧地落在地上。\n\n其他表演者也纷纷效仿，在不断变化的虫洞中编织和捕捉彼此的复杂图案。当一名杂技演员滑进一个传送门，在虫洞关闭时切断了他们的一条肢体时，人群倒吸了一口气。就在他们要着地的时候，受伤的演员又从另一个虫洞里消失了。\n\n断臂从椽子上掉下来，抓住了一个空中秋千，然后自己飞向空中。这位独臂杂技演员再次出现，跳得很高，在半空中重新接上假肢，然后降落在其他表演者的金字塔上，最后进行了盛大的表演。\n\n人群显得兴高采烈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

No sooner had we taken our places then a troupe of vulpine mammalians in sparkling costumes appeared on-stage. After a short display of advanced gymnastics, a small wormhole appeared in the center of the stage. One daring acrobat dove inside just before the hole winked out of existence. Another wormhole appeared high above the crowd, ejecting the performer who deftly tumbled to the ground.\n\nThe other performers followed suit, weaving and catching one another in complex patterns through an ever-shifting series of wormholes. The crowd gasped as one of the acrobats slipped into a portal, severing a limb from their body as the wormhole closed. Just as they were about to hit the ground, the wounded performer disappeared through yet another wormhole.\n\nThe severed arm dropped from the rafters, grabbing a trapeze as it fell and flinging itself into the air. The one-armed acrobat reappeared, leapt high, reattached their prosthetic in mid-air, and landed on a pyramid of the other performers in a grand finale.\n\nThe crowd was very pleased.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:637`

## astral_rift.2910.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2910.a&lang=en)

### 中文

高端科技的离谱用法……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

A flippant use of impressive technology...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:638`

## astral_rift.2915.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2915.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生命之颚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

The Jaws of Life

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:640`

## astral_rift.2915.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2915.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个巨大的爬行类义体人笨重地走到舞台中央。他带着巨大的金属重物，轻巧地扔进自己巨大的下颚。随着一声液压嘶嘶声，它把重物一个接一个地抛向空中，在它们下落时接住它们，然后毫不费力地把它们抛出去。\n\n一声响亮的通告使一些观众吓了一跳。一个有很多触手的机器人、一个双足实体、还有一团浓密的藤蔓被带到舞台上。然后，这些生物心甘情愿地跳进爬行类机器人的嘴里，让自己以同样的方式被巧妙地玩弄。\n\n然后，这只机械野兽把三个人含在嘴里，蹲坐在地上，把自己紧紧压在地上。它巨大的框架发出熔化般的橙色光芒。过程完成后，表演者张开嘴，只有一只生物走了出来。它有触手机器人的球状头部，类毒素的躯干，以及由缠绕的藤蔓组成的粗壮四肢。它向人群做了个手势，引起了一阵震惊的沉默。\n\n然后，半机械人重新吃下了这只奇美拉，并再次吐出了三个人，完好如新。人群紧张的沉默突然变成了欢呼声。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

A large, reptilian cyborg lumbered into center stage. It carried huge metal weights, which it deftly tossed into its gargantuan jaw. With a hydraulic hiss, it spat the weights into the air one after another, catching them as they fell and launching them once again in an effortless juggle.\n\nAn announcement blared, causing some of the audience to jump. A many-tentacled robot, a bipedal entity, and a living tangle of thick vines were brought to the stage. These creatures then willingly jumped into the reptilian cyborg's mouth, allowing themselves to be deftly juggled in the same manner.\n\nThe mechanical beast then held the three volunteers in its mouth and squatted, compressing itself against the floor. Its huge frame glowed a molten orange. Once the process was complete, the performer opened its mouth and only a single creature walked out. It had the bulbous head of the tentacled robot, the torso of the toxoid, and thick limbs made of intertwined vines. It gestured to the crowd, evoking a stunned silence.\n\nThe cyborg then re-ingested the biological amalgam and spat out the three individuals once again, good as new. The crowd's nervous silence erupted into cheers.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:641`

## astral_rift.2915.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2915.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是有趣的技艺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

An interesting talent.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:642`

## astral_rift.2920.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2920.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最终表演

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

The Final Act

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:644`

## astral_rift.2920.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2920.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一幕涉的主角是一头由厚厚的灰色石头组成的生物。它弯曲成奇怪的姿势，把它那块状的四肢扭曲成不可思议的结。然后它高高跃上空中，用它宽大的指尖着地，人群中发出了礼貌的窃窃私语。然而，这只是真正性能的冰山一角。\n\n生物的运动开始缓慢减速，直到时间本身开始逆转。它的身影沿着上次跳跃的弧线折回，停在半空中。人们几乎惊得跳了起来。\n\n突然间，停滞状态被打破了。那个生物回到了地面，开始变形。它的身体从毫无特色的灰色石头变成了闪闪发光的红色水晶、自由流动的沙子、脆弱的蓝色页岩等等。它继续做着一系列更加复杂的扭曲动作。\n\n在它的性能最高的时候，它爆裂成了成千上万的棱柱形碎片。这些碎片以一种复杂的方式冻结在半空中，将舞台上简单的聚光灯折射成一种令人难以置信的色彩和图案的复杂展示。\n\n碎片慢慢地绕回来，直到表演者完好无损地回来，这头怪物离开了舞台，人群中发出了无与伦比的欢呼声。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

The final act involved a creature made of a thick gray stone. Bending into peculiar positions, it contorted its blocky limbs into impossible knots. It then jumped high into the air and landed on its wide fingertips, to polite murmurs from the crowd. However, this was only a setup to the true performance.\n\nThe creature's movements began to slowly decelerate, to the point at which time itself began to reverse. Its figure traced back along the arc of its previous jump and froze in mid-air. This brought the crowd to life.\n\nSuddenly, the stasis was broken. The creature returned to the ground and began to transform. Its body changed from featureless gray stone to sparkling red crystal, free-flowing sand, brittle blue shale, and more. It continued to perform an ever more complicated series of contortions.\n\nAt the height of its performance, it burst into thousands of prismatic shards. These froze mid-air in an intricate arrangement, refracting the stage's simple spotlight into a complex display of incredible colors and patterns.\n\nThe shards slowly rewound until the performer was returned whole, and the creature left the stage to an unmatched roar from the crowd.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:645`

## astral_rift.2920.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2920.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是稀罕的生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

A rare specimen, indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:646`

## astral_rift.2925.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2925.name&lang=en)

### 中文

演出之后

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

After the Show

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:648`

## astral_rift.2925.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2925.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

演出结束了，但是观众们被邀请留下来与其中一个演员近距离接触。这将是更详细地研究个体的绝佳机会，因为他们每个人都表现出了我们甚至无法复制的能力。\n\n每个表演者都非常受欢迎，所以我们可能只有时间在车站关门前见其中一个。我们应该优先考虑哪个提议?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

The show is over, but the audience was invited to stick around to meet one of the acts. It would be an excellent opportunity to study the individuals in more detail, as each of them displayed abilities we cannot even begin to replicate.\n\nEvery performer is tremendously popular, so we may only have time to see one before the station closes. Which act should we prioritize?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:649`

## astral_rift.2930.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2930.name&lang=en)

### 中文

会见杂技家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Meet the Acrobats

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:654`

## astral_rift.2930.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2930.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

狐类杂技演员大约一米高，三角形的耳朵不时地抽动。其他身体特征包括浅黄色的皮毛，灵巧的前爪和弯曲的假拇指，以及短而下垂的尾巴。当它们用尖锐的外星舌头兴奋地喋喋不休时，我们可以看到它们坚硬扁平的牙齿，这意味着它们是食草动物。\n\n杂技演员们与不同的观众进行了热烈的互动。由于粉丝的数量相对较少，他们能够与每一位粉丝进行生动的对话，表演特定的虫洞技巧和体操表演以获得小费。\n\n它们是一群才华横溢的生物，有许多有价值的特性值得研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

The vulpine acrobats stand at roughly a meter tall with triangular ears that twitch occasionally. Other physical characteristics include pale yellow fur, dextrous forepaws with a curved false thumb, and short hanging tails. When they chatter excitedly in their sharp alien tongue, we can see their tough, flat teeth, implying a herbivorous diet.\n\nThe acrobats interact energetically with the various members of the audience. Due to having a relatively smaller number of admirers, they are able to engage with every fan in animated conversation, performing specific wormhole tricks and gymnastic feats for donations.\n\nThey are a talented group of specimens, with many valuable properties to study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:655`

会见大嘴

### English

Meet The Maw

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:659`

## astral_rift.2935.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2935.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个爬行类义体人几乎有一艘小型运输船的高度和宽度，它的嘴巴大得几乎也可以把那么大的船吞下去。其他的物理特征包括：灰色的皮肤上贴着透明的电子面板，躯干上固定着一个发光的能量核心，合金金属四肢配有复杂的液压系统。\n\n考虑到它巨大的尺寸，这个半机械人像一座纪念碑一样牢牢地固定在它的位置上。许多粉丝给它带来了吃的，它似乎很喜欢。自愿参加演出的外星人也在场，作为表演的一部分故作姿态。\n\n这个庞大的生物应该会开启各种各样的研究可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

The reptilian cyborg stands at the height and width of a small transport ship, with a mouth large enough to nearly swallow it as well. Other physical characteristics include gray-scaled skin patched with transparent electrical panels, a glowing energy core fastened to its torso, and alloyed metal limbs complemented with a complex system of hydraulics.\n\nGiven its enormous size, the cyborg is anchored solidly to its spot and is regarded as if it were a monument. Many fans have brought the monolith something to eat, which it seems to appreciate. The aliens that volunteered for the show are also present, posturing as part of the act.\n\nThis gargantuan specimen should open up a variety of research possibilities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:660`

## astral_rift.2940.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2940.name&lang=en)

### 中文

会见柔术家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Meet the Contortionist

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:664`

## astral_rift.2940.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2940.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

柔术演员高高站在拥挤的人群中，灰色的石头组成粗壮的四肢隐藏着一种柔韧的感觉。它的躯干非常纤细，有划痕、凹痕和碎片，与它们完美无瑕的混凝土般的附属物形成鲜明对比。\n\n这位柔术演员并不是特别健谈，但他会接受别人的要求来展示自己的变形能力，尤其是对年轻的仰慕者或出手阔绰的人。\n\n作为这个热门节目的主角，我们有机会从这个物种身上学到很多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

The contortionist stands tall among the crowded assembly, with thick limbs of gray stone that belie a hidden flexibility. Their torso is remarkably slender, marked with scratches, dents, and chips, in stark contrast to their flawless concrete appendages.\n\nThe contortionist is not particularly talkative, but is taking requests to show off their metamorphic abilities, especially for younger admirers or big tippers.\n\nAs the headline act in this popular show, we have an opportunity to learn a great deal from this specimen.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:665`

## astral_rift.2960.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2960.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高维度基因

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Extra-Dimensional Genetics

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:669`

## astral_rift.2960.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2960.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以进一步调查杂技演员了。[leader.GetName]以松散的毛囊和丢弃的绷带的形式秘密收集数据样本。似乎没有一个表演者在意我们的行为。他们可能已经习惯了在这段时间里仰慕者的怪异行为。\n\n这些样本果然非常有趣的：根据我们的分析，它们的狐狸基因被某种超然的来源改变了。可以观察到他们的水晶血细胞在空间和时间点之间伸展。不管这对健康有什么负面影响，它能让物种成员以自然的方式操纵虫洞。这一发现将彻底改变我们对遗传学和生物生命的神奇潜力的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

We were able to investigate the acrobats more closely. [leader.GetName] surreptitiously collected data samples in the form of loose hair follicles and discarded bandages. None of the performers seemed concerned by our behavior. They may be used to admirers acting peculiarly during these sessions.\n\nThe samples proved to be extremely interesting: according to our analysis, their vulpine genetics have been altered by some sort of otherworldly source. Their crystalline blood cells can be observed stretching between points in space and time.  Whatever negative health effects this may have, it allows members of the species to manipulate wormholes in natural fashion. This discovery will revolutionize our understanding of genetics and the marvelous potential of biological life.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:670`

## astral_rift.2960.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2960.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

给予一项基因科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Gives a Genetic technology.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:671`

## astral_rift.2965.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2965.name&lang=en)

### 中文

微型虫洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Micro-Wormholes

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:673`

## astral_rift.2965.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2965.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

部分得益于我们的大笔捐款，我们能够以更个人的身份与杂技演员见面。表演者非常感激，并展示了各种令人印象深刻的客厅技巧，包括微虫洞——从把小物体切成两半到创造一组无限循环的成对入口。\n\n我们能够记录这一独特的现象以供进一步研究，并且相信这些演示将大大加深我们对超空间技术的理解。诚然，他们对待这种危险的物理学的随意性也着实令人震惊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

We were able to meet the acrobats in a more personal capacity, helped in part by our significant donation. The performers were very appreciative and demonstrated a variety of impressive parlor tricks involving micro-wormholes - from slicing small objects in half to creating an infinitely looping set of paired portals.\n\nWe were able to document this unique phenomenon for further study, and are confident these demonstrations will greatly deepen our understanding of hyperspace technology. Admittedly, the casual nature with which they approach such dangerous physics was alarming to witness.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:674`

## astral_rift.2970.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2970.name&lang=en)

### 中文

非正式样品收集

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Unorthodox Sample Collection

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:676`

## astral_rift.2970.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2970.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们试图偷偷地收集一小块爬行类义体人的组织时，它生气了。随后，它向我们的外派团队喷出了一波粘稠的绿色粘液，我们被安保无人机送出了该设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

In our attempts to surreptitiously gather a small piece of the reptilian cyborg's tissue, we angered the creature. It subsequently vomited a wave of viscous, green slime onto our away team and we were escorted out of the facility by security drones.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:677`

## astral_rift.2970.desc.organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2970.desc.organic&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2970.desc.common$\n\n奇怪的是，我们观察到这种黏液对我们的[leader.species.GetSkinType]有显著的愈合作用。小小的伤口和擦伤干净利落地愈合了，留下了一种愉快的感觉。这种生物致密的液体可以用作各种药物和治疗方法的溶剂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

$astral_rift.2970.desc.common$\n\nStrangely enough, the slime was observed to have remarkable healing properties on our [leader.species.GetSkinType]. Small wounds and abrasions cleanly knitted themselves whole and left behind a pleasant sensation. This biologically dense fluid could be used as a solvent for a variety of medicinal and curative therapies.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:678`

## astral_rift.2970.desc.inorganic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2970.desc.inorganic&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.2970.desc.common$\n\n粘稠的流体充满了生物材料，似乎适合用作高营养食品凝胶的基础。我们正在利用它的特性从有机废物中开发一种类似的消化剂，这将大大提高我们食品生产系统的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

$astral_rift.2970.desc.common$\n\nThe viscous fluid was dense with biological material, and seems suitable for use as a basis for a highly nutritious food gel. We are using its properties to develop a similar alimentary paste out of organic waste which should vastly increase the efficiency of our food production systems.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:679`

## astral_rift.2975.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2975.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

部分因为捐赠了大量的食物，我们能够以更个人的身份与庞大的义体人见面。\n\n这只大嘴巴的动物异常热情，兴奋地转过身来。它花了很多分钟完成这个360度旋转，让我们对它的义体控制增强和支持系统进行了深入的研究。\n\n从我们的分析中，我们能够确定它曾经是由一只典型的智慧爬行动物物种构成的，现在它的大部分质量都归功于强化组件的安装。鉴于更新的数量庞大，且许多遭受到了明显的氧化，这个个体应该已经活了几个世纪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

We were able to meet the massive cyborg in a more personal capacity, helped in part by our significant donation of food.\n\nThe wide-mouthed creature was uncharacteristically enthusiastic and turned itself around in excitement. It spent many minutes completing this 360 degree spin, allowing us an in-depth cataloging of its cyborg's cybernetic enhancements and support systems.\n\nFrom our analysis, we were able to determine that they had once been a fraction of its current size as a typical sapient reptilian species and owes most of its mass to their augmentations. Given the huge number of upgrades in various conditions of oxidation, this individual must have been alive for centuries.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:683`

### English

## astral_rift.2980.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2980.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们挤过人群，能够更近距离地观察那位柔术演员。\n\n他们向我们展示了一只沉重的石头手臂，毫不费力地把它变成了一堆不同的矿物。最初粗糙的花岗岩变成了暗红色的宝石，最后变成了闪闪发光的晶体。材料的移动将细小的尘埃颗粒送入空气中，我们很快就将其作为样本收集下来。\n\n这些样品含有一种挥发性生物物质，可以在非常特殊的条件下使某些矿物质自发结晶。这种材料的大规模使用可以利用到我们现有的矿物生产中，并达到类似的效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

Pushing ourselves through the crowds, we were able to investigate the contortionist more closely.\n\nThey displayed for us one of their heavy stone arms, effortlessly transforming it into a cluster of different minerals. What began as coarse granite shifted into dull red gemstones, and finally sparkling crystals. The shifting of the material sent fine particles of dust into the air, which we were quick to secure as a sample.\n\nThe samples contain a volatile biological substance that can be used to spontaneously crystallize certain minerals under very specific conditions. The use of this material on a large scale could be leveraged into our existing mineral production and achieve similar effects.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:687`

量子矿化

### English

Quantum Mineralization

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:690`

## astral_rift.2985.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.2985.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

部分得益于我们的大额捐款，我们能够以更个人的身份与柔术演员会面。\n\n这个生物的物质似乎并不完全受我们现实的束缚。仔细观察后，它告诉我们，它是如何改变复合材料的磁性排列的，这样一来，通往其他维度的小开口就形成了足够长的长度，可以把自己的原子与来自另一个时间和地点的原子交换出来。有了这样的量子重新排列机制，很难说这个生物是否和我们几个小时前遇到的完全一样。\n\n虽然这样的能力确实让人印象深刻，但我们一想到这个物种的整个文明能取得什么成就，就会不寒而栗。尽管如此，这是一个宝贵的研究机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

We were able to meet the contortionist in a more personal capacity, helped in part by our significant donation.\n\nThe material of this creature did not seem wholly bound to this reality. On closer inspection, it shared with us how it could shift the magnetic alignments of its composite materials in such a way that small openings into other dimensions formed just long enough to literally swap its own atoms out with those from another time and place. With such quantum realignments, it is difficult to know if this being is the same as we encountered only hours ago.\n\nWhile such an ability does make for an impressive performance, we shudder to think what an entire civilization of this species could achieve. It has been a valuable research opportunity, nonetheless.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_2_l_english.yml:691`
